# Supported Formats & Technical Reference

This document contains detailed technical notes, reverse-engineering findings, and the complete format capability registry for **Wiimms SZS Tools Plus**.

---

## Format Capability Index

| Format | Platform / Category | Decode | Encode | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **AJJPG / AJPG** | GBA / Still Image | ✅ | ✅ | GBA-era still image container |
| **ADJB** | Switch / Model sidecar | ✅ | ❌ | Smash Ultimate mesh triangle adjacency (model.adjb): per-mesh id + u16 index lists, sized like Adjb.cs |
| **AFS** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Sega/CRI-style flat archive reused by Spike Co.'s engine for *Dragon Ball Z: Budokai Tenkaichi 3* (`wzs3us0/1/2.afs`); magic `AFS\0` + u32 LE file count, then that many `{offset,size}` u32 LE pairs, one trailing pair pointing at a 48-byte-per-entry name/date metadata table; decode-only, verified against retail samples by direct hexdump; CREATE repacks `<name>.afs.d` (names from the `NNNN_name` prefix, 0x800 alignment, zeroed timestamps) |
| **SCB / IMB / PLB** | DS / Background | ✅ | ❌ | Luminous Arc 2 screen maps with same-stem tiles and palette; 250 retail PNGs checked, [audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **LZE** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Luminous Arc 2 `Le` streams, four token forms, re-encoded greedily by `wszst compress <file> -d <name>.lze`; 1,275 retail resources verified against CUE, [audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **IEAR** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Luminous Arc MAIN/JTBL typed resource archives; indexed payload extraction, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **ALAR** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Jump Stars type 2 / type 3 named archives, including nested DSCP wrappers; [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **DAD** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Monolith Soft / Procyon Studio *Soma Bringer* LZSS compression (`DAD\x01`), re-encoded greedily by `wszst compress <file> -d <name>.dad`; 20/20 retail archives decompressed cleanly, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **OBP** | DS / Graphics | ✅ | ❌ | Monolith Soft *Soma Bringer* sprite/object tile banks with RGB555 palette (`OBP1`); structured inspection via `wszst text` / `dump`, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **BGP** | DS / Graphics | ✅ | ❌ | Monolith Soft *Soma Bringer* 256x192 background tile maps with RGB555 palette (`BGP1`); structured inspection via `wszst text` / `dump`, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **PCS** | DS / Animation | ✅ | ❌ | Monolith Soft *Soma Bringer* 2D layout and animation keyframe sequence (`pcs\0`); structured inspection via `wszst text` / `dump`, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **SPR** | DS / Animation & Sprites | ✅ | ❌ | Konami *Castlevania* DS sprite object definition (`0xBEEFF00D`, *Dawn of Sorrow*, *Portrait of Ruin*, *Order of Ecclesia*); parts, hitboxes, frames, frame delays, and animation tracks disassembled to text via `wszst text` / `dump`, 512/512 retail files verified, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **CPAC** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Capcom *Ghost Trick: Phantom Detective* / *Resident Evil: Deadly Silence* multi-section archive container (`cpac_2d.bin` / `cpac_3d.bin`); tagged sections (`BKEY` models/animations/textures, `PKEY` palettes), LZ11 auto-decompression, 26,000+ retail members verified, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **CING-WPF** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | CiNG Wish Pack File (*Hotel Dusk: Room 215* / *Wish Room*, `.wpf` / `.tbl`); contiguous 32-byte directory records with 24-byte filename, 32-bit payload size, and 32-bit offset stride to next member; extraction via `wszst extract` and creation via `wszst create`, 275 retail archives and 6,605 members verified byte-for-byte, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **JUPITER-PCK** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Jupiter Corp Nintendo DS Model/Motion Package (*The World Ends with You*, *Kingdom Hearts: Re:coded*, `.pck`); header length, member count, and 32-bit entry sizes table followed by Nitro 3D resources (`BMD0`, `BCA0`, `BTP0`, `BTX0`, `BTA0`, `BMA0`, `COLI`); 90 retail archives verified byte-for-byte, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md); CREATE repacks `<name>.pck.d` in file-name order |
| **RL** | GBA/DS / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Nintendo 8-bit Run Length compression (magic `0x30`), including wrapped containers (e.g. Professor Layton / Level-5 DS `.arc` / `.arj` with method code 1); decompressed via `wszst decompress`, 58/58 retail Layton 1 samples verified byte-for-byte, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md); re-encode with `wszst compress <file> -d <name>.rl` |
| **L5-PAC** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Level-5 / Armor Project PAC archive container (`.pac` / `.dat`, *Dragon Quest IX: Sentinels of the Starry Skies*); contiguous 80-byte member headers with declared filename, uncompressed payload size, and allocation stride; 316 retail archives and 1,475 member files verified, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **SSZL** | Wii / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Namco Museum Remix LZSS0 compressed stream (`SSZL` magic, `.lzs`); decompression via `wszst decompress`, extraction via `wszst extract`, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **VCRA** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Namco Museum Remix resource archive (`VCRA` magic, `.vcra` / `.bin`); extracted via `wszst extract` and created via `wszst create`, 56-byte member names, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md) |
| **INIO-LZO** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | iNiS LZO1X compressed stream (`1OZL` magic, *Osu! Tatakae! Ouendan*, *Moero! Nekketsu Rhythm Damashii*, *Elite Beat Agents*); 12-byte header with decompressed and compressed sizes; verified across 15,702 retail files byte-for-byte, [retail audit](FORMAT_AUDIT.md); re-encode (M3-only LZO1X, valid but not size-optimal) with `wszst compress <file> -d <name>.1ozl` |
| **THOR** | Wii / Resource package | ✅ | ❌ | Behaviour Interactive resource package (`.wii`, *Phineas and Ferb: Quest for Cool Stuff*; the v3 hash-bucket layout also in *Happy Feet* and *Alvin and the Chipmunks: Chipwrecked*, whose 110/112-bucket tables end at the first list; Alvin's 960-byte `.wil` files are empty packages) -- not a disc image; 0x40-byte header with an ASCII "Thor" marker at 0x3C, a mostly-empty run of 8-byte BE (key,val) slots ending in a `{0xFFFFFFFF,count}`/`{table_offset,0}` marker pair, then `count` 12-byte BE `{id,offset,size}` resource records; many blobs carry a small `0xABABABAB`-prefixed envelope with an embedded name used for extraction, others are dumped raw by index; decode-only, verified against all 85 retail samples by direct byte analysis. A second layout ("v3", *Happy Feet* (Wii), header bytes `03 02 02 0d`) replaces the record table with 256 8-byte BE hash buckets `{count, list offset}` at 0x40 (empty = `{0, 0xFFFFFFFF}`) pointing at `{id, offset}` entries; sizes are implied by the next higher offset, `0xFFFFFFxx` offsets are unresolved references and are skipped, and members that are nested v3 packages are written as `.wii` so extraction recurses (335 of the 693 retail `.wii` files carry members, the other 358 are empty 2 KB shells; 91 of 118 members in `bn_tee06.wii` are 96-byte `proxy:*` stubs, names from the `0xABABABAB` envelope with `:` written as `_`) |
| **ALZ1** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Arika 4096-byte window LZSS with inverted flag bits |
| **Arika (INFO.DAT/GAME.DAT)** | DS/DSi / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Obfuscated directory decryption and member decompression |
| **ARCV** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Pac-Man Party (Wii) archive; byte-exact round-trip |
| **ART / IMG** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Monster Games GUI image format |
| **ASH0** | GameCube/Wii / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Nintendo ASH0 compression (System Menu, Animal Crossing, My Pokémon Ranch; automatic 11/15-bit distance tree fallback) |
| **AGI** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Toys for Bob "AGI" archive (`\x1AAGI`, *Skylanders: Swap Force* `files/*.pak`): big-endian header + backslash-path name table; extract-only. Two entry-table record layouts, tried in order: plain 2-word `(offset,size)` pairs (pure audio-bank `.pak` files) and 4-word `(0, offset, unk, flag)` groups whose on-disk size is derived from the gap to the next block-aligned offset (mixed audio + `.igz`/`.igx` model/material `.pak` files). Verified against all 2339 retail `.pak` files sampled from the Swap Force DATA partition: 100% parse successfully (1559 via the 2-word layout, 780 via the 4-word layout). Raw `FSB5` audio members are tagged `.fsb` and auto-decoded to WAV via the existing FSB4/FSB5 decoder; `.igz`/`.igx` model/material members are extracted raw (IGZ internals out of scope) |
| **AT7** | PS2/Wii / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Koei Tecmo container |
| **BCBNK** | 3DS / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | NW4C bank: wave table and instrument slot list to YAML |
| **3DS Camera PACK** | 3DS / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | `.pack` table of 0x40-byte `{name[0x38], offset, size}` records; the LZ11 members are extracted as they are; CREATE repacks `<name>.pack.d` (16-byte aligned members) |
| **GBIN** | 3DS / Data | ✅ | ✅ | Camera guide table (`GUID` records with named parts) to YAML; `wszst create <file>.gbin.yaml` rebuilds the binary (names up to 16 characters; trailing bytes of the original are not kept) |
| **BCFNT** | 3DS / Font | ✅ | ✅ | 3DS bitmap font to PNG atlas |
| **BCH** | 3DS / Model | ✅ | ✅ | CTR H3D model container |
| **BCLAN** | 3DS / Layout | ✅ | ✅ | 3DS layout animation |
| **BCLIM** | 3DS / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | CTR image container |
| **BCLYT** | 3DS / Layout | ✅ | ✅ | 3DS binary layout |
| **BCWSD** | 3DS / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | NW4C wave sound data: wave table and wave sound list to YAML |
| **BCRES** | 3DS / Model | ✅ | ✅ | CTR graphics container |
| **BCSAR** | 3DS / Audio Archive | ✅ | ✅ | CTR Sound Archive (CSAR) |
| **BCWAV** | 3DS / Audio | ✅ | ✅ | CTR Sound Wave |
| **BCWAR** | 3DS / Audio Archive | ✅ | ✅ | CTR Sound Wave Archive (CWAR) |
| **BCGRP** | 3DS / Audio Archive | ✅ | ✅ | CTR Sound Group Archive (CGRP) |
| **BFFNT** | Wii U / Font | ✅ | ✅ | Wii U bitmap font to PNG atlas |
| **BG4** | 3DS / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Mario & Luigi flat archive with BLZ member compression |
| **BIGF** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | EA BIGF / BIG4 container (`.big`). Extraction reads all 224 *SSX Blur* archives: little- or big-endian total size, `\` or `/` member paths, table ends with an `L2nn` marker (`L234`, `L231`, `L222`) or zero padding. Encoding writes the layout of most retail files (header field = end of the name table, 4 zero bytes, members on 128-byte boundaries): 170 of the 215 *SSX Blur* BIGF files re-create byte-exact; the others (older tool layouts, BIG4) round-trip with identical members |
| **BFLAN** | Wii U / Switch / Layout | ✅ | ✅ | Wii U layout animation; `wszst xx` also writes `<file>.bflan.xml` for Switch FLAN members (all 2,187 in *Tomodachi Life: Living the Dream* decode; before, `xx` only converted BRLAN animations) |
| **BFLIM** | Wii U / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Wii U textures (BC1-BC5) |
| **BFLYT** | Wii U / Switch / Layout | ✅ | 🟡 | Wii U binary layout. Switch FLYT 8.x/9.x materials (flags before colors) are decoded: the color block {u8 type mask, u8 count, u8 offsets} is skipped to its end before the texture refs (it used to be read as texref 0, failing 342 of the 544 *Tomodachi Life: Living the Dream* layouts), mapping sources 5-7 and combiner modes 12-15 are accepted, the flag-19 block (28 bytes + 16 per combiner stage, before the projection mappings) is kept as hex under `detailed-combiner`, and unknown trailing data (flag bits 20 / 24) as `unparsed`. All 544 layouts decode. Encoding still writes the Wii U material order, so Switch layouts do not re-encode byte-exact. |
| **BFRES** | Wii U / Switch / Model | ✅ | ✅ | GX2/NX model container (passthrough / extraction). Switch: skeleton-only models export their joints; an animation-only archive (FSKA list, no FMDL, e.g. *Tomodachi Life: Living the Dream* `Model/AnimationProduct.bfres`, 2,150 clips) is bound by `wmdlt DECODE` / `wszst xx` to the first `*Skeleton*.bfres` (or `.bfres.zs`) next to it that matches; *Ocarina of Time* (Switch 2) `<Base>_Animation.anim.bfres` clips are bound to the sibling model `<Base>.<Base>.bfres(.zs)` first. FSKA Euler-mode rotations (flag 0x1000) are converted to quaternions and bones holding only base values (static poses) are kept. `wszst xx` writes AAMP external files (e.g. AGL `Default.baglofx` in `.bofx` / `.genvres`) as `<name>.yml`. |
| **BFSAR** | Wii U / Switch / Audio Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Sound Archive (FSAR) |
| **BFWAV** | Wii U / Switch / Audio | ✅ | ✅ | Sound Wave |
| **BFWAR** | Wii U / Switch / Audio Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Sound Wave Archive (FWAR) |
| **BFGRP** | Wii U / Switch / Audio Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Sound Group Archive (FGRP) |
| **MWT** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Planet-G engine "GDATAVERSION" texture envelope (`.MWT`; *Bermuda Triangle - Saving the Coral*, *Rock Blast*, *Veggy World*, *Ocean Commander*, *Arcade Shooting Gallery*): 0x40-byte header (version 2, payload size, width, height, flags), a standard TPL and, with flags bit 0, a 1-bit hit mask of ceil(w / 8) x h bytes; file size checked exactly on all 3,807 retail samples of those five discs. `xx` writes `<file>.MWT.png` beside the source (all 1,162 *Rock Blast* textures decode), also for the members of `.PKI` packs. Plain-TPL `.MWT` files are handled as TPL |
| **PLANETG** | Wii / Sprites | ✅ | ❌ | Planet-G sprite resource (`.MWG`/`.MSP`; *Bermuda Triangle - Saving the Coral*, *Rock Blast*, *Veggy World*, *Ocean Commander*, *Arcade Shooting Gallery*): root type `PLANETG_SPRITECOLLECTION` version 2 (`.MWG`, a list of optional sprites) or `PLANETG_SPRITE` version 1 (`.MSP`, one sprite). Layout taken from the SpriteCollection / Sprite / SpriteObj / SpritePicture readers in *Rock Blast*'s `main.dol`: a sprite is a name plus named animations, an animation a list of optional objects, an object a name plus three key tracks with their own time lists (pictures: `.MWT` path, 0xRRGGBB colour, flag, optional LTRB rect, w/h; positions: x/y floats; values: 0/1 floats) and 5 flag words. All 435 `.MWG`/`.MSP` files of the five games parse to the exact end of file; `wszst xx` writes the tree as `<file>.txt`. Meaning of the value track and the 5 flags is not confirmed |
| **PKI** | Wii / Container | ✅ | ❌ | Planet-G "IMAGE_WII_COMPACT_FILE_VERSION_1" pack of `.MWT` files (`.PKI`; *Bermuda Triangle*, *Ocean Commander*): name/size/offset entry table, every entry inside the file behind the table (24 retail samples) |
| **PGF** | Wii / Font | ✅ | ❌ | Planet-G bitmap font (`.pgf`; the five games above and *Offshore Tycoon*): family, point size, padding, x/y/w/h tables for 256 codes and the atlas size (not always a power of two), then a run-length tail (`u8` run, or `0` + `u32`) covering the atlas. Each run is one colour of the font's texture (a separate `.MWT`, e.g. `TAHOMA16.MWT`), but the colour is not stored: two-colour atlases (9 of 21 samples, identical alpha to their `.MWT`) decode to `<file>.png`, white ink on transparent; anti-aliased or shadowed ones only mark colour changes and keep the metrics text (their image is the `.MWT`). Detection requires the glyph rectangles to lie inside the atlas (21 retail samples) |
| **RKET** | Wii / Scene | ✅ | ❌ | Eko Software "RKET" resource container (`.rck` scenes, `.spa` language packs; *Aqua Panic!*, *SPRay*, *Cyberbike - Cycling Sports*, *The Garfield Show*, *Bratz Kidz*, *Drawsome!*, *Adibou*): 56-byte header ending in eight 0xAF bytes, `file_size == 56 + payload_size` when the payload is stored; checked on 156 retail samples from three discs. Payload not decoded |
| **MATF** | Wii / Material | ✅ | ❌ | Eko Software "MATF" material chunk table (`.mat`; *Aqua Panic!*, *SPRay*, *Cyberbike - Cycling Sports*, *The Garfield Show*, *Bratz Kidz*, *Drawsome!*, *Adibou*): header plus `MAT `/`TEX ` chunks running exactly to the end of the file (136 retail samples); chunk payloads not decoded. Other games' `.mat` files are not claimed |
| **MB2** | Wii / Metadata | ✅ | ❌ | Eko Software "BNAM" bone/node name table (`.mb2`; *Aqua Panic!*, *SPRay*, *Cyberbike - Cycling Sports*, *The Garfield Show*, *Bratz Kidz*, *Drawsome!*, *Adibou*): header plus length-prefixed NUL-terminated names running exactly to the end of the file (136 retail samples); fully decoded |
| **VISLIST** | Wii / Visibility | ✅ | ❌ | Eko Software per-object visibility lists (`.vis`, no magic; *Aqua Panic!*, *SPRay*, *Cyberbike - Cycling Sports*, *The Garfield Show*, *Bratz Kidz*, *Drawsome!*, *Adibou*): one block per object, `{u32 groups, u32 total, groups x {u32 group, u32 count, count x {u32 object, u32 value}}}`, accepted only when the walk ends exactly at the end of the file (136 retail samples, 24 bytes to 856 KB); fully decoded. NW4R `VIS0` animations keep the `VIS` name |
| **LIT** | Wii / Lighting | ✅ | ❌ | Eko Software light list (`.lit`; *Aqua Panic!*, *SPRay*, *Cyberbike - Cycling Sports*, *The Garfield Show*, *Bratz Kidz*, *Drawsome!*, *Adibou*): tag 0x20031126, count, 36-byte lights `{u32 id, u32 0, f32 x, y, z, f32 r, g, b, f32}`, `file_size == 12 + 36 x count` (136 retail samples, 0-16 lights); fully decoded. Other games' `.lit` files are not claimed |
| **BLZ** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Nitro backward-LZSS |
| **BPE / GFCP** | Wii / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Good-Feel Byte Pair Encoding (GFAC mode 1) |
| **BMD** | DS / Model | ✅ | ✅ | Early Nitro 3D models |
| **J3DBMD** | GameCube / Wii / Model | ✅ | ✅ | J3D binary model (`J3D2bmd3`, legacy `bmd2`); SuperBMD-compatible GLB round-trip (geometry, skinning, materials, GX textures incl. mipmaps; canonical single-TEV encode, RGBA32/CMPR, triangle lists) + material/texheader JSON sidecars + `--profile` |
| **J3DBDL** | GameCube / Wii / Model | ✅ | ✅ | J3D binary display list (`J3D2bdl4`); same coverage as J3DBMD, MDL3 section written as a parseable stub (prefer BMD for in-game use) |
| **BNTX** | Switch / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Switch texture container (Tegra block-linear); native DDS/ASTC block export |
| **BREFT** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Brawl effect texture |
| **BRFNA / BRFNT** | Wii / Font | ✅ | ✅ | NW4R font to PNG atlas + XML metrics |
| **BRLAN / BRLYT** | Wii / Layout | ✅ | ✅ | NW4R layout and animations |
| **BRRES (MDL0, TEX0)** | Wii / Graphics | ✅ | ✅ | NW4R models, textures, animations; a textured MDL0 object with no UV set (an environment map on the Wii, e.g. the *Animal Crossing: City Folk* balloons) gets sphere-map UVs baked from its bind-pose normals so its texture shows in the GLB |
| **BRSAR** | Wii / Audio Archive | ✅ | ✅ | NW4R sound archive |
| **BRSTM / BFSTM / BCSTM** | Wii/Wii U/3DS / Audio Stream | ✅ | ✅ | Multichannel ADPCM/PCM streams; `xx` decodes them natively to `<name>.wav` (loop kept in a `smpl` chunk, sample-identical to mobipeg), mobipeg only remains as fallback |
| **BYAML / BYML** | Wii U / Switch / Data | ✅ | ✅ | Binary YAML format (versions 1–7, big- and little-endian, string/path/hash tables incl. reloc maps); path tables ride any version, not just v1; Yaz0-wrapped BotW containers (`.sbyml`, `.smubin`, …) decode transparently and re-compress on CREATE; encoder honors `.be`/`.vN` dest markers and `# byml version=N endian=..` headers; `wszst BYMLFIND` searches keys/values |
| **CA01 / SA01** | 3DS / Wii U / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Mii Maker & amiibo settings flat archives |
| **CCF** | Wii / Switch / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Virtual Console container |
| **CGFX** | 3DS / Model | ✅ | ✅ | CTR NW4C model container |
| **CRAM (.arc)** | 3DS / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Xenoblade Chronicles 3D archive |
| **CSB** | Switch/Wii U / Collision | ✅ | ✅ | Paper Mario collision scene (TTYD/Origami King LE, Color Splash BE): triangle meshes with material/collision flags + sphere/box trigger volumes, GLB round-trip with generated `.ctb` |
| **CS_DCT** | Wii / Archive + Graphics | ✅ | ❌ | *Chicken Shoot* (Wii) stage archive (`.dct`): background planes (LZ-compressed indexed pixels + RGB555 palette) → PNG, animated sprites with per-frame bounding geometry → PNG, object placement metadata → `manifest.json` and raw binaries |
| **CTB** | Switch/Wii U / Collision | ✅ | ❌ | Paper Mario collision search table (XZ-quadtree over `.csb` triangles; regenerated on encode, inspected with `wmdlt CAT`) |
| **CTPK** | 3DS / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | CTR texture container |
| **DARC** | 3DS / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Differential archive container |
| **DSB (TXTR)** | DS / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Animal Crossing: Wild World menu texture (RGB555 palette + A3I5 texels); retail regression covers decode → encode → decode pixels |
| **DSP** | GameCube / Wii / Audio | ✅ | ✅ | Nintendo GameCube/Wii DSP-ADPCM standalone audio stream (`.dsp`): mono/stereo ADPCM coefficients, nibble predictor/scale decoding → 16-bit PCM WAV; `wszst compress <mono 16-bit>.wav -d <name>.dsp` encodes a mono, unlooped stream (stereo and loop points not supported) |
| **Retro TXTR** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Retro Studios texture (*Metroid Prime 1-3*, *DKCR*): GX-tiled, indexed + direct |
| **Tropical TXTR** | Wii U / Texture | ✅ | ❌ | Retro Studios texture (*Tropical Freeze*): RFRM form, GX2 detile |
| **DAT (Star Fox Zero)** | Wii U / Archive | ✅ | 🟡 | Big-endian flat archive |
| **Deflate** | Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Standard Deflate / Zlib streams |
| **DTLS (dt00/ls00)** | Wii U / 3DS / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Super Smash Bros. 4 composite resource package & lookup (Wii U `of02` 16-byte + 3DS `of01` 12-byte retail variants included) |
| **FSYS** | GameCube / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | Genius Sonority Pokémon archive |
| **ZIP / TSZIP / SZIP / ZGS** | Multi-platform / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Stored and DEFLATE members, CRC/length validation before extraction, bounded streaming output; no encryption, ZIP64, or split archives Also read from `.szip` (the *Super Swing Golf* shadow maps, plain ZIPs).; `.zgs` (*Pangya! Golf with Style* GSR texture packs) are plain ZIPs |
| **FZIP** | Wii U / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Game & Wario Zlib container |
| **GFA** | 3DS / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | GFAC archive |
| **GF1MOT** | 3DS / Animation | ✅ | ❌ | Game Freak XY/ORAS bone-motion pack (SPICA GF1MotionPack): skeleton + per-anim frame/octet listing as text |
| **GFLX** | Switch / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Game Freak GFLXPack archive (SPICA GFLXPack): raw-LZ4 members, magic-sniffed extensions |
| **GFMOT** | 3DS / Animation | ✅ | ❌ | Game Freak skeletal/material/visibility motion (SPICA GFMotion): frames, tracks and constants as text |
| **GFMPACK** | 3DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Game Freak model/texture/shader pack (SPICA GFModelPack): named members with sniffed extensions |
| **GFMODEL** | 3DS / Model | ✅ | ❌ | Game Freak GFL2 3D model (SPICA GFModel, Pokémon X/Y/ORAS): skeleton, materials with texture names, PICA200 command-buffer geometry with smooth skinning → GLB |
| **GFPKG** | 3DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Game Freak Gen6/Gen7 package (SPICA GFPackage): offset-table members with sniffed extensions |
| **GFTEX** | 3DS / Texture | ✅ | ◐ | Game Freak GFL2 texture (SPICA GFTexture): PICA200 payloads incl. ETC1 → PNG (`wimgt decode`); an edited `<file>.gftex.png` next to the `.gftex` is re-encoded in place by `wszst create` in every PICA format (RGBA8 / RGB8 / RGB565 / RGBA5551 / RGBA4 / LA8 / HiLo8 / L8 / A8 / LA4 / L4 / A4 / ETC1 / ETC1A4, ETC1 with a simple two-mode encoder). Whether PICA images are stored bottom-up could not be checked without a real file |
| **MBN** | 3DS / Model | ✅ | ❌ | ModelBinary companion buffers (SPICA MBn): replacement vertex/index buffers applied onto the sibling `.bch` base scene → GLB; descriptor listing as text standalone |
| **MTMFX** | 3DS / Shader | ✅ | ❌ | Capcom MT Framework Mobile shader effects (SPICA MTShaderEffects): input-layout table (vertex attribute names/formats/offsets) as text; layouts drive `wmdlt` MOD decoding |
| **MTMOD** | 3DS / Model | ✅ | ❌ | Capcom MT Framework Mobile model (SPICA MTModel): skeleton always; vertices via sibling `.mfx`/`.lfx` input layouts (else validated float fallback); materials/textures via sibling `.mrl` → GLB |
| **MTMRL** | 3DS / Material | ✅ | ❌ | Capcom MT Framework Mobile materials (SPICA MTMaterials): CRC32-keyed texture bindings as text |
| **MTTEX** | 3DS / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Capcom MT Framework Mobile texture (SPICA MTTexture): PICA200 payloads incl. ETC1 → PNG; `wimgt copy <file>.png -d <name>.mttex` writes a new RGBA8 texture (version 0xa3 header, one mip; other formats such as ETC1 are not encoded) |
| **GTX** | Wii U / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Wii U GX2 texture container |
| **GSH** | Wii U / Shader | ✅ | ✅ | Wii U Latte GPU shader container |
| **HSD (.dat)** | GameCube / Model | ✅ | ✅ | HAL Laboratory sysdolphin object graph; also archive bundles (many archives in one file, `scene_data` roots, e.g. Doraemon `map*_dat.mdl`) merged into one GLB |
| **OCN. (.dat)** | Wii / Model | ✅ | ❌ | Paon "OCN." skinned model (Donkey Kong: Barrel Blast `chara/*.dat`, LZ10 wrapped): TEXS textures, bones, GX display lists over a per-vertex multi-bone WEIG pool (s16 or f32 layouts), rigid shapes from float ELEM banks, each MESH's material index at +0x14; exported as one skinned GLB with PNG textures. `*_mp*.dat` are FJG tables of HSD motion archives |
| **Paon .d4** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | *Donkey Kong: Barrel Blast* `.d4` (`chara/spheremap.d4`, `system/load*.d4`, `strap*.d4`, `gimmick/gp_*.d4`): u16 GX format, u16 width, u16 height, u16 0, u32 pixel offset (0x20), then one GX image (CMPR or RGBA8 in the retail files; heights need not fill the last block); `wszst xx` writes `<name>.png` beside it |
| **HSF** | GameCube / Wii / Model | ✅ | ✅ | Hudson Mario Party 3D model |
| **IPK** | Wii / Wii U / Switch / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Ubisoft UbiArt archive (*Just Dance*, *Rayman Origins/Legends*): stored + zlib/LZMA members, old/new path-name orders |
| **SEDB (.rps / txb / shd / lyb / PHB)** | Wii / Archive + Texture | ✅ | ❌ | Square Enix resource container (*Dragon Quest X* `layout/bin/wii/*.rps`): RES index with per-member names split into `.txb` / `.shd` / `.phb` / `.mtb` / nested `.rps`; `txb` textures (GX CMPR, linear LA8 / RGBA8, 256-colour palette) decoded to PNG; `.shd` materials (named float parameters), `.lyb` layout databases (string pool) and `.phb` collision blobs (bounds, block count) summarised to `<name>.txt`. Recognised by `wszst FILETYPE` as `SEDB`. Full `.lyb` object graphs and `.phb` collision geometry are not decoded |
| **WADH** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Data Design Interactive `DataWII.wad` (*Ninjabread Man*): stored members, directory tree via last-child / previous-sibling links; members whose data lies past the end of the archive (56 hash-named `.bmp` entries in *Anubis II*) are skipped |
| **DC2 DCX / DCT** | Wii / Archive + Texture | ✅ | ❌ | *Jakers! Kart Racing*: `.dcx` directory archives (backslash paths, contiguous members); `.dct` GX CMPR / RGBA8 textures (top mip) → PNG |
| **DIG** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Bomberman Land* `cddata*.dig` streaming resource package: magic-less, extension-resolved; flat table fills the first 0x800 bytes (12-byte header, entries start at byte 16), 16-byte big-endian records `(offset_sectors, size_sectors, unknown, reserved)`, sector = 2048 bytes (`<< 11`); resolved offset/size verified against real samples by confirming embedded Nintendo TPL texture headers (`00 20 AF 30`) land inside every resolved range across all 10 supported real files. No on-disk filenames (numbered-blob output); 5 of 15 real files use an unsupported nested "type == 2" table and are rejected by `ScanDIG()` rather than mis-extracted. |
| **NIF (Gamebryo 20.6, Wii)** | Wii / Model + Texture | ✅ | ❌ | *Pocoyo Racing*: big-endian `.nif` scene graph; `NiMesh` GX display-list / INDEX streams → GLB in world space (skinned meshes in bind pose, hidden collision meshes only when nothing else exists); embedded DXT1/RGBA `NiPersistentSrcTextureRendererData` → PNG |
| **Grip RES** | Wii / Archive + Texture | ✅ | ❌ | *Sesame Street: Elmo's Musical Monsterpiece*: `res\n` serialised resource packages split into per-section files (`strings.txt`, `index.txt`, `.fsb` sound banks, compiled Lua, raw object sections); `surf` textures (CMPR / RGB5A3 / I8, bottom-up rows) → PNG. Model object graphs (`body`/`bmsh`/`gshd`) are pointer-linked and stay as raw sections |
| **Goliath GS Package** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | **Older dialect / wrapper:** *The Amazing Spider-Man* (Wii) `.pkz` files start with `BA BE B1 B0` (BE: block size, data offset, unknown, block count, file size, uncompressed total, then `count` cumulative uncompressed end offsets and a per-slice index table); each `0x8000`-aligned block from `data_offset` is an independent raw zlib stream with no final-block bit, inflated to `end[i]-end[i-1]` bytes. The result is a GS package of the older dialect: 12-byte chunk header (`id/version/has_children/size`, no `size_hi`, ids without the high bit, root `0x00000001`), name field at `0x18` (not `0x1c`), 44-byte texture header, and pixels pooled in `0x26` wrappers whose own `0x138e` name record carries the same leading hash as the owning resource, so pairing is by hash (the pool is not in document order). Formats 2/3/4 are the same RGB5A3/CMPR/CMPR+I8 chains. Verified: 234/234 retail packages extract, 12,294 PNGs. Newer dialect: *Skylanders: SuperChargers Racing* `files/Data/*.pkz`: a self-checking big-endian chunk tree (uniform 16-byte `id/version/has_children/size_hi/size` header, `0x80000001` root, every level consumes its parent's payload exactly). Named resources are `0x8000138d` wrappers whose first child (`0x8000138e`) carries a hash + NUL-terminated name; bulk texture (`0x80000195`) and audio (`0x80001134`) payloads are pooled elsewhere in the tree and matched to their `0x80000197`/`0x80001133` descriptions positionally, in document order (exact across all 782 retail files). Textures are GameCube-native mip chains (CMPR, RGB5A3, or CMPR+I8-alpha) re-wrapped as one-image TPLs → PNG; format 5 and mismatched-size RGB5A3 headers are declined rather than guessed. Audio is RIFX WAVE with wFormatTag 2 carrying a Nintendo DSP-ADPCM extension (not MS-ADPCM despite the tag): per-channel coefficient/gain/history state is re-packed as standalone `.dsp` streams and decoded through the existing DSP-ADPCM path (stereo → two mono `.dsp`, de-interleaved). Extract-only; geometry/materials/scripts/engine runtime tables are not interpreted. Verified against all 782 retail packages: 782/782 walk cleanly (586496 chunks, 216 distinct (id,version,children) combinations), 760/782 yield extractable content, 7031/7055 textures decode (99.66%, 19 format-5 + 5 size-mismatched RGB5A3 declined), 4994/4994 audio streams decode, 73900 names recovered |
| **CDGaCube CAR** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Cat Daddy Games `birthday.CAR` (*Birthday Party Bash*): 24-byte records, 2048-byte sector offsets, trailing path table; members stored raw or as bare zlib streams (inflated on extract) |
| **Terminal Reality POD** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | POD3 / POD4 / POD5 (*Nickelodeon Dance* `WII*.POD`): stored members with backslash paths (converted to `/`); compressed POD4 members are skipped. Layout after jopadan/termpod |
| **Z-Axis ZSND (GCUB)** | Wii / Audio bank | ✅ | ❌ | `ZSNDGCUB` sound banks (`.zss`/`.zsm`; *Marvel: Ultimate Alliance 2*, 306 files; layout after vgmstream): each stream's per-channel DSP headers and 0x8000-byte interleaved data are split into `NNNN[_chN].dsp` (mono, stereo, four-channel), which `xx` decodes to WAV. 228 sounds from five banks are sample-exact against vgmstream; only the GCUB platform variant is handled |
| **Tecmo MAD/LAD/EFE/CLF pack** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Resource packs of *Rygar: The Battle of Argus* (`.mad` maps, `.lad` actors, `.efe` effects, `.clf`; 419 files): an 8-byte pack tag, a member count and a table of absolute offsets (unsorted, 0 = empty); members (`tgs`, `XMDF`, `texa`, `ORB`, `COLI`, ...) are written as `NNNN_tag.bin` and cover every file exactly; the members themselves are not parsed. Unrelated to EA's PS2 `.mad` video |
| **STME `.stm`** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Streaming environment packages of *Need for Speed: Hot Pursuit* (24 files, 580-1,350 records each): `STME` + `WI01`, a record count and 0x40-byte records {kind, name, data offset, size, header size}; every record is written as `NNNN_name` (`.mp1` / `.tex` pairs point at the same bytes), and the extracted `.tex` textures are decoded to PNG by the existing texture decoders. Models and the other kinds are not interpreted |
| **Exient `.bnk`** | Wii / Audio bank | ✅ | ❌ | Sound banks of *Need for Speed: Hot Pursuit* (`u32` count + `{offset, size, 0}` table of complete `WIIADPCM` streams): split into `NNNN.adpcm`, which the WIIADPCM extractor turns into per-channel DSP / WAV. All 7 members of the sampled bank decode sample-exact against vgmstream |
| **Exient `.twi`** | Wii / Texture | ❌ | ✅ | `TWI\0` textures of *Need for Speed: Hot Pursuit* (24 files; width/height/mip count/format header, 0x20-byte header): the GX data is decoded to `<file>.png` with `xx` for IA8, RGBA8, CMPR and the C4 / C8 paletted variants (RGB5A3 palette stored before the data). Only the base mip level is written; the format ids are inferred from the level sizes and checked visually, there is no reference decoder |
| **Super Fruit Fall `.tdw`, `.mee`** | Wii / Texture, text | ❌ | ✅ | `.tdw` = `u32 count` then per texture a 0x40-byte name, `u32` GX format (4 RGB565, 5 RGB5A3, 6 RGBA8, 14 CMPR), `3`, `0`, width, height, data size, the GX tiled data and 4 pad bytes (`loadscreen.tdw`, `saveicon.tdw`): each texture is decoded to `<file>.<name>.png` with `xx` and checked visually. `.mee` = little-endian localisation table (`u32 count, u32 key bytes, u32 text bytes`, or the 24-byte head variant `0, count, key bytes, 0, text bytes, 0`) followed by the NUL-separated `TID_*` keys and then the Latin-1 texts, written to `<file>.d/strings.tsv` (all 10 `lang*.mee` / `*strings.mee` tables of the disc). `code.mee` (`MEEX`) is not decoded |
| **DLS `.dls`** | Wii / Audio bank | ❌ | ✅ | RIFF `DLS ` Downloadable Sounds banks, notably `sound/Roland/GM16.DLS` (3.4 MB, 495 mono 16-bit 22.05 kHz waves, `INFO/INAM` names such as `BASSN69`), shipped unchanged by about 20 budget titles (*Ninjabread Man*, *Monster Trux Arenas*, *Kidz Sports*, *Rig Racer 2*, ...). Every `LIST wave` of the `wvpl` pool is written to `<file>.d/<index>[_<name>].wav` with its `fmt ` chunk copied verbatim; all 495 decode cleanly in ffmpeg. Instruments, regions and loops (`lins`, `ptbl`, `wsmp`) are not exported. The sibling `gm16pcm.wt`/`gm16adpcm.wt`/`.pcm` files (a Nintendo wave table of the same samples) are left as is. Generic Nintendo compression detection no longer treats RIFF files as CX00-wrapped streams (a tiny WAV whose size byte was `0x24` used to produce a bogus `.bin`) |
| **Racquet Sports `.SGC` speech bank** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `MUSIC/DIALOG/NN.SGC` (BE u16 1, u32 count, 16-byte entries {hash, rate, 0x0800, offset, size}; unsigned 8-bit mono PCM) → `<file>.d/NNNN_<hash>.wav` (11 clips in `01.SGC`). |
| **K11 `.btx` dialog table / `.a2` texture** | Wii / Text, Image | ✅ | ◐ | `wii/Text/<LANG>/*.btx` (BE entry count, entries of {id, field count, fields of {kind 1 speaker / 2 line / 3 emotion, UTF-16BE text}}) → `<file>.tsv`; `wii/Characters/Textures/*.a2` (BE GX format, width, height, mip count, first-level size, GX-tiled data from 0x1c) → `<file>.png` (verified: 512×512 CMPR clothing texture).; the texture / banner (not the text table) takes an edited PNG on repack (header kept)|

| **Standalone `.pcm` DSP-ADPCM sounds** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `*.pcm` files that are complete Nintendo DSP-ADPCM files (0x60-byte BE header, e.g. *Alan Hansen's Sports Challenge* `SFX/**/*.pcm`, 1,250 files, 48 kHz) → `.wav` (only when the whole file passes the DSP header check; `.wt`-paired `.pcm` banks are unaffected). |
| **EA Tiger Woods `.sas` RefPack stream chain** | Wii / Compression | ✅ | ❌ | `data/stmfiles/*.sas` (swing-capture streams): back-to-back RefPack (`10 fb`) chunks on 0x800 boundaries → concatenated `<file>.sas.dec` (verified: `femsac.sas`, 569 chunks, 38.5 MB; the record layout inside is not decoded). |
| **EA SNR EALayer3 stream (`.snr`)** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | *FaceBreaker K.O. Party* `audio/music/loadloops/*.snr`: EAAC header (`05 04 <rate>`, codec 5 = EALayer3), blocks `{flags\|size, samples, payload}`; the granule stream is re-assembled to MPEG-2 Layer III like the SCHl EALayer3 path → `.mp3` (+ WAV with `--decode`). The first ~1 KB before the first run of valid frames is skipped. Verified on two loop files (stereo 24 kHz, music-like decode); single-stream `.mus` files (zero-padded 0x100 index + the same stream, e.g. `venue_2a_ram020.mus`) decode the same way; the large multi-track `.mus` files were not checked. |
| **EA AEMS (`S10S`) EA-XAS stream** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | *FaceBreaker K.O. Party* `audio/aems/*.aems`: packets from 0x40 `{u32 flags\|size, u32 samples/channel, payload}` (flag 0x80 = control), payload = EA-XAS v1 0x4c-byte blocks (4 group headers + 15 rows of 4 data bytes, 128 samples/block, channel blocks alternate) → `<file>.wav`. The rate is not stored; 32 kHz is assumed (the final flag-0x80 packet is decoded too). Verified: `fe_sfx_stream.aems` (smooth stereo audio) and `beannouncer_stream.aems` (24.2 M samples of announcer speech bursts). |
| **EA multi-track `.mus` (EA-XAS)** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | *FaceBreaker K.O. Party* `audio/music/*.mus` with 16-byte EAAC codec-4 stream headers at 0x400 (rate + sample count per track) and the tracks as the same packet chain as `.aems` from 0x600 → `<file>.d/NNNN.wav` (verified: `boxers02.mus`, all 28 tracks, 32 kHz stereo, decoded sample counts equal the header counts). |

| **Little League TPC (`.tpc`)** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ◐ | *Little League World Series Baseball 2009* `bin/**/*.tpc` texture packs: `{0, count, 0, 0}` + 32-byte entries {texture offset, palette offset, height, width, ..., GX format, palette entries, palette format}; CI4/CI8 (palette IA8/RGB565/RGB5A3), RGB565, RGB5A3 and RGBA8 are decoded to `<file>.d/NN.png` (all 698 retail files decode). |
| **Marines WWB (`.wwb`)** | Wii / Audio archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Marines: Modern Urban Combat* `Marines.wwb`: little-endian count + `{offset, size}` table; the 468 sounds (28-byte header with sample count and rate 22050 Hz, then ADPCM data) are split to `<file>.d/NNNN.snd`; the audio itself is not decoded. |
| **Mercury Meltdown PAQ (`.paq`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Mercury Meltdown Revolution* `general.paq`: big-endian `{2001, count, data start, 0}` + 16-byte entries `{name hash, size, offset, 0}`; no names, so members are `NNNN_<hash>.<ext>` (`.arc` U.8 archives and AT7 members are recursed; `TSPA`/`FNT1` resources are written raw). |
| **Nicktoons MLB BCT (`.bct`)** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | *Nickelodeon Nicktoons MLB* `data/models/stadiums/*/*_envmap.bct`: 0x30-byte big-endian header (`06`, width @4, height @6, data size @0x1c) + CMPR texture with mips; the top mip is wrapped as `<file>.tpl` and decodes to PNG with `--decode` (sphere-map environment textures, 256² or 512²).; edited PNG re-injected on repack (header kept, mip chain regenerated)|
| **Planet 51 S3T (`.s3t`)** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | *Planet 51: The Game* `textures/*.s3t`: big-endian `{GX format, width, height, 0, 0, 0, 0x201, 0}` + untiled-header GX image data (RGB5A3 in all samples); wrapped as `<file>.tpl` and decoded to PNG with `--decode`. Images are stored bottom-up (the PNG comes out vertically flipped).; edited PNG re-injected on repack (header kept, mip chain regenerated)|
| **Purr Pals RSC0 (`.rsc`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Purr Pals* `*.rsc` resource containers: `RSC0` + blocks `{tag, length, name hash, ...}` (TEX0/GRP0/STR0/SND0/ANM0/MDL0/COL0/BIN0/FNT0), either back to back from 0x20 or listed in a 16-byte `{tag, hash, offset, size}` directory (e.g. `objects.rsc`). TEX0 textures (RGBA8 `0x17xx` / CMPR `0x18xx`, low byte = mip count) become `NNN_TEX0_<hash>.tpl` and decode to PNG with `--decode`; `GRP0` groups recurse into subdirectories; mono `SND0` blocks (flags `0x100000`: 16 DSP coefficients, a 0x20-byte stub, then 8-byte DSP-ADPCM frames; rate in the first half-word at +0x18) are rewritten as `NNN_SND0_<hash>.dsp` and decode to WAV with `--decode` (verified on `habanera.rsc`: 73 s at 32 kHz); every other block is written raw as `NNN_<tag>_<hash>.bin` without decoding (streamed `SND0` stubs, flags `0x200000`, 0x60 bytes in `stream.rsc`/`streamvoc.rsc`/`sound.rsc`, carry only the size: their data follows the block table at the offset in the header, one stream after another, each 0x20-aligned, and is decoded the same way; verified on `stream.rsc` (5 streams), `streamvoc.rsc` (9 streams) and `sound.rsc` (414 effects; 32/48 kHz, 5 noisy short effects)). |
| **Ubisoft LySE wrapper (`.wav`)** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | *Just Dance 2* `WD2.wii.wav.bf` members (119 files from the ABE v5 BigFile): a `LySE` header in front of a standard 16-bit PCM RIFF/WAVE (32 kHz stereo); the RIFF part is written as `<name>_riff.wav`. |
| **Luxor PPK (`.ppk`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Luxor: Pharaoh's Challenge* `ASSETS_WII/*.PPK` (`PRPK`, little-endian): 64-byte nodes from 0x30 {crc, flags (bit 16 = directory), parent index, data offset @0x10 (0x80-aligned), size @0x14, name @0x18}; the directory tree (`ASSETS_WII/MAPS/<level>/...`) is reproduced; contents (`.B1T` textures, `PATH.OBJ`, `RULES.TXT`) are written as stored and not decoded further. |
| **Line Rider ACP (`.acp`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Line Rider Freestyle* `Media/Art/*.ACP`: big-endian count + settings, then a table of `count + 1` offsets (last = file size) that ends where the first `LRMH` chunk starts; the chunks (one model per frame/variant, 77 KB each in `Target.ACP`) are split to `<file>.d/NN.lrmh` and not decoded further. |
| **Monster Jam BigFile (`MJ.bft`/`.bff`/`.bfd`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Monster Jam: Path of Destruction* `MJ.bft` index (be32 count, {offset, size}; offset bit 31 = pointer to {n, n offsets} of duplicate copies, first used) with full paths from `MJ.bff` and data in `MJ.bfd` → `<file>.d/<path>` (5963 of 5970 files; 7 are empty placeholders). The `MJ.bfh` 16-byte name hashes are not needed. Contents: FSB4 sound banks, TPL, `.mti`/`.bin`/`.crv` data |
| **Crystal Dynamics BigFile (`.wii-w`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Tomb Raider: Anniversary* `bigfile.000.wii-w` and `chapterN.000.wii-w` (be32 count, ascending name hashes, 16-byte `{size, aux, locale mask, 0}` records; data from the next 0x800 boundary, entries padded to 0x800, records with identical `{size, aux}` share one copy; the layout is only accepted if it ends exactly at the end of the file) → `<file>.d/NNNN_<hash>[_<locale>].raw\|bin` (2113 and 509 files). Names exist only as hashes; `!WAR` chunk files get `.raw` |
| **G.I. Joe level pack (`.lvl_NNN`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *G.I. Joe: The Rise of Cobra* `JOE/Wii/levels/gui.lvl_740` (be32 data start, be32 count, 0x80-byte header, then `{name hash, offset, size, type}` 16-byte entries) → `<file>.d/NNNN_<hash>_t<type>.bin` (149 non-empty of 166 entries, 51 MB; contents not decoded further). The sibling `.lvlrmp`/`.mmpa`/`.gui_*` files are not handled |
| **Tony Hawk: Ride `.txw`** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | `flash/frontend/.../dynamicimages/*.txw` (0x40-byte header `AF AF 00 02 … BEEFFACE`, u16 type, u16 width/height at +0x14, then GX CMPR data) → `<file>.tpl` + PNG (640×448 verified visually). Type-2 files hold one CMPR image; type-4 files hold two stacked CMPR images and only the first is decoded; edited PNG re-injected on repack (header kept, mip chain regenerated)|
| **Sega ABRS pack (`.abr`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Sakura Wars: So Long, My Love* `DISK1/SLG/*/E/*.abr` (little-endian `ABRS` header with a chunk-offset table; the chunks are walked through their own `{tag, payload size, header size}` headers up to the `EOFC` trailer) → `<file>.d/NNN.<tag>` (`hmdl` models, `htex` textures, `hmot` motions, `hmrp` …; verified on ~100 files, empty packs are skipped). The chunk payloads are not decoded further |
| **Deadly Creatures `.wii.database`** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `Database/-Level- ChapterN.wii.database` (big-endian `LMXB` blocks with zlib tables and Lua 5.1 bytecode, then embedded sound banks): the little-endian FMOD `FSB4` banks (545 in Chapter 10, 116 MB) are carved to `<file>.d/NNNN.fsb` and decoded by the FSB handler. The `LMXB` tables, Lua scripts and non-audio resources are not extracted |
| **LZMA-wrapped BRRES (`.brtex`)** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | *TV Show King Party* `data/*.brtex` (also in *We Sing* and *Showtime Championship Boxing*): `FF 'LZMA' 00` + LZMA-alone stream → `<file>.brres`, which is then extracted normally (verified on `splash_169.brtex`: 385 KB → 2 MB BRRES with NW4R textures); re-encode with `wszst compress <file> -d <name>.brtex` |
| **Epic Mickey 2 `.chf` pack** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Disney Epic Mickey 2* `animations.chf` (be32 magic `85620240`, be32 count, 0x20-byte header, `{id, offset, size}` 12-byte entries, 0x10-aligned data ending exactly at EOF) → `<file>.d/NNNN_<id>.bin` (7598 files verified). Contents are animation data, not decoded further |
| **Marvelous `pBin` pack (`.dpt` …)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Harvest Moon: Animal Parade* `cg/3d/dun/dpt/dpt_d.dpt` (be32 `pBin`, 1, 1, `FFFFFFFF`, table offset `0x20`, count; 16-byte `{tag, 0, size, offset}` entries with tags `FMS `/`TEX `/`UVM `; data to the end of the file) → `<file>.d/NNN_<tag>.bin` (119 members verified on one file; the members are `FIPA`-wrapped and not decoded further). Distinct from the Koopatlas `kpbin` and Mario `pbin` handlers |
| **Sega `gos1` pack (`.gos`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Ghost Squad* `mdl/objset_*.gos` (be32 size-ish, `gos1`, be16 count, offset table ending where the first member starts, members back to back to EOF; each member starts with its NUL-terminated name) → `<file>.d/NNN_<name>.bin` (77 members verified on `objset_stage3e.gos`). Member contents (models) not decoded. The sibling `.gms` motion packs (be32 count, count offsets, members to EOF) are extracted to `<file>.d/NNNN.bin` as well (`char_mot.gms`: 886 members, `NGIF` data) |
| Arc Rise Fantasia `.vld` | `Btl/btl_*.vld`: sequence of `RTDP` archives (be header size, count, total; 0x28-byte `{name[0x20], size, offset}` entries), 0x800-aligned, tiling the file. Extracted to `<file>.d/NNN_<stem>/<member>`. |
| Kidz Bop Dance Party `.tpk` | Texture pack: be32 0, be32 0x10, zero-terminated ascending offsets of `bres` files (length at +8). Extracted to `<file>.d/NNNN.brres` and recursed. |
| Disney Wreck-It Ralph `.glob` | `GLOB` pack: be32 entry count+1, data start at +12, 12-byte `{id, offset, size}` entries from 0x14 (terminator id `7fffffff` ends at EOF). Extracted to `<file>.d/NNNN_<id>.bin`. |
| WordJong Party `.rvli` | `rvli`/`rvif`/`rvpx` image: be32 width/height/bpp at +0x14..+0x1c, GX-tiled pixels from 0x40 (32 bpp = RGBA32, 16 bpp = RGB5A3). Wrapped as `<file>.tpl` and decoded to PNG with `xx`. |
| Titeuf - Le Film `.PAKz` / `.PAK` | `zst1` + zlib wrapper (from +12) around a `TATE` pack: le32 size, le32 count, 0x100 header, then 0x80-aligned `item` entries `{"item", size, index, 0, name[0x70]}` + data. Members extracted to `<file>.d/<name>` (backslashes become `/`; duplicate names overwrite). |
| Dragon Ball Tenkaichi Daibouken `.RBIN` | be32 section count n, 12 zero bytes, n+1 ascending be32 offsets from 0x10 (last = file size). Sections written to `<file>.d/NN.bin` and recursed (they are CMPS tables). |
| Resident Evil (GC remake) `.emg` / `.emd` | Leading list of ascending 0x20-aligned be32 section offsets (zero terminated; last section to EOF or final offset = file size). Sections written to `<file>.d/NN.bin` (not decoded further). |
| Resident Evil 4 (Wii) `.drs` / `.das` | 0x20-byte title, 0x20-byte block entries `{type, size, 0, offset}` from +0x20 (terminator type `ffffffff`), first block at 0x400, blocks contiguous to EOF. Written to `<file>.d/NN_<type>.bin`; type 4 blocks are nested containers of the same layout and are recursed. |
| G.I. Joe: The Rise of Cobra `GamePak*.dfu` | be32 count + 5 header words (24 bytes), then 16-byte `{aux, name hash, offset, size}` entries, 0x800-aligned data. Streamed to `<file>.d/NNNN_<hash>.bin`. |
| The Price Is Right `.uNN` | Plain ZIP archives under non-zip names (`Update.u00` .. `Update_3.u50`): any file with a `PK\x03\x04` header that no native extractor claims is unpacked through 7-Zip. |
| Ford Racing - Off Road `.wig` | `graphics.wig`: le32 inflated size + zlib stream; inflated data = le32 count, 12 zero bytes, 16-byte `{le32 offset, name[12]}` entries and images at `0x10+16*count+offset` (0x40-byte header each, not decoded). Written to `<file>.d/<name>.img`. |
| FreeStyleGames `FSG-FILE-SYSTEM` (DJ Hero 2 `DISC0.IMG`, `.part0`..`.partN`) | Multi-part image: header, `{path hash, type, record offset}` nodes, `{offset>>10, size}` records, `D`/`F` name lists from the data base 0x20000; real paths are recovered by hashing. Parts are concatenated automatically; entries in missing parts are skipped. Layout per the public GameArchives (maxton) reader. |
| Dead Rising: Chop Till You Drop (Wii) `.efa` | Effect archive (`\0AFE`, header size 0x40): groups of `{count, 12 zero bytes, 24-byte {offset, size, name[16]} entries}` back to back; group 0 holds BRRES files (recursed), later groups `KTPP` parameter blobs. Written to `<file>.d/GG_NNN_<name>.<ext>`. |
| EA Black Box `JDLZ` (NFS Nitro `.lzc`, any file with the magic) | `JDLZ`, 02 10 00 00, le32 inflated size, le32 compressed size, dual flag-byte LZ (f1 literal/match, f2 match encoding). Decompressed to `<file>.bin` and recursed. Algorithm per the public open-nfs-bin-viewer. |
| Open Season `.umd` (Unreal UMOD) and `.lin` | `.umd`: 20-byte footer `{0x9fe3c5a3, directory offset, size, version, crc}`, compact-index directory of `{name, offset, size, flags, filetime}`; only unique 16-byte-aligned entries are real members (406 of 802 in the Wii build, the rest are compiled placeholders). `.lin`: chain of `{le32 inflated, le32 compressed, zlib}` chunks, inflated to `<file>.pkg` (Unreal package). `.liv` is not decoded. Layout per the ResHax Open Season thread. |
| Samurai Shodown Anthology `.acx` | Sound bank: be32 0, be32 count, `{offset, size}` entries from +8, data follows the table. Members written to `<file>.d/NNN.bin` (not decoded). |
| Detective Conan (Korea) `.hst` | `HST\0`, be32 1, be32 image size, then a plain TPL from +0x0c. Written to `<file>.tpl` (decoded to PNG with `xx`). |
| Family Game Night 4 `.blk` | `blockfiles/*.blk`: 0x20-byte header (be16 1, be16 1, be16 count), 0x9c-byte entries `{name[0x80], offset, size, flags, hash[16]}`, 0x20-aligned data tiling to EOF. Members written to `<file>.d/<name>`. |
| Tom Clancy's H.A.W.X. 2 `.ccd` | be32 size, be32 count n, be32 0x1c, n ascending record offsets from +0x1c; each record starts with a be32 pointer to its name in the string pool after the last record. Records written to `<file>.d/NNNN_<name>.bin` (not decoded further). |
| Epic Mickey 2 `.nif_wii` | Gamebryo NIF version 20.6.5.0 (same layout as the supported 20.6.0.0 files): the embedded GX texture is decoded to `texture.png` beside the file. |
| Spectrobes: Origins `DATA.LXHD` + `DATA.LXBD` | Genki LXTools FileCombine: `FMXL` header file with 0x20-byte entries `{flags, padded size, 0, offset, 0, path offset, inflated size, stored size}` (paths are stored in the header); compressed members are `RPCL`/`LLZ7` + LZ11. Extracted with full `DATA/...` paths; needs the `.LXBD` beside the `.LXHD` (members past the end of a partial `.LXBD` are skipped). |
| Looksley's Line Up `.5bg` (NTBG) | LE `NTBG` + `0xfeff 0x100`, size, then `PALT` (u32 n, BGR555 colours) and `BGDT` (u8 depth 0 = 4 bpp with 16-colour sub-palettes / 1 = 8 bpp, screen bytes, w/h in tiles, tile bytes; 32-wide NDS text-BG map, 8x8 tiles). Decoded to `<file>.png` (xx); colour 0 transparent. Checked against 11 retail files. `DFPL` ignored. |
| Our House - Party! `.wtpak` | be32 crc, be32 1, be32 count, be32 ?, `{size, offset, name hash}` entries from +0x10, contiguous data to EOF. Streamed to `<file>.d/NNNN_<hash>.bin`. |

| **EA pathfinder overlay `.mus` (Spore Hero EALayer3)** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | *Spore Hero* `audio/rwac/pathfinder/*/ovl_*.mus`: the same table style (12-byte `{stream offset, aux, index<<16}` records, last record index 0) but every stream is an EAAC codec-5 (EALayer3) SNR stream → `<file>.mp3`, `<file>.N.mp3` (+ WAV with `--decode`). Verified on `sporefe/ovl_010.mus` (6 streams) and `ovl_020.mus` (3): decoded durations are within ~2% of the header sample counts; 3 of the 6 streams in `ovl_010` look like music (high autocorrelation) and the others like noisy effects. `sporefe/strm_music.mus` (546 tracks) also decodes.
