Version-1 catalogs (Zhu Zhu Pets: Wild Bunch `ZZP_Text.txtc`, 280 keys x 5 languages) have no extra-key count and decode the same way. `.fmod` files use the same compression but hold an audio-event name catalog (not decoded). |
| **Rockstar Table Tennis `.spd`/`.spt` sound banks** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `zaudio/WiiBanks/*.spd` + `.spt` (BE `{count, count x 28-byte headers {0, rate, 0, 0, end nibble, start nibble, 0}, count x 46-byte blocks {16 coefficients, gain/ps/history}}`, absolute nibble addresses into the headerless DSP-ADPCM `.spd`) → `<file>.d/NNNN.dsp` + WAV (verified: FEMUSIC_01 music 24 kHz 86.7 s, AMB_UN_JAPAN 2 ambience tracks 44.1 kHz, VO_SHANE 78 voice clips 32 kHz; waveforms smooth/plausible, not listened to). |
| **Star Trek: Conquest `.WSB` + `.WSS` sound banks** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `Audio/**/*.WSB` (BE header + 0x40-byte sound records `{offset, nibbles, 2, rate, 16 coefficients, ps, ...}` at the end of the file) with the headerless DSP-ADPCM `.WSS` → `<file>.d/NNNN.dsp` + WAV (verified: DIALOGUE/CAR.WSB, 257 voice clips at 22.05 kHz; waveforms smooth, speech-like amplitudes, not listened to). |
| **Harvest Moon: Animal Parade `.stb` (STBL)** | Wii / Text | ✅ | ❌ | `msg/*.stb`, `event/*.stb` (BE `{STBL, ver, entries, width, entry table, data}`; 16-byte `{id, 0, count, offset}` entries of fixed-width UTF-16BE strings) → `<file>.tsv` (verified: formMsg.stb, 14 strings). `SHOP`/`SCA `/`OPAL`/`EVTP`/`GRID` sibling formats remain undecoded. |
| **Humongous Entertainment `.he2` speech bank** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | Freddi Fish `game/*.HE2` (`TLKB` block of `TALK` blocks with `HSHD` header and `SDAT` unsigned 8-bit PCM) → `<file>.d/NNNN.wav` (1,246 clips in `FREDDI.HE2`, 11 kHz). The `.HE0` index (XOR 0x69 `MAXS`/`DIRI`…) and `.HE4` music (`SONG`/`SGHD`/`SGEN`) are not decoded. |
| **Humongous Entertainment `.(a)` game resources** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `game/<GAME>.(a)` (whole file XOR 0x69; decoded `LECF` > `LFLF` rooms > resource blocks) → the `DIGI` sounds (`HSHD` + `SDAT`, unsigned 8-bit PCM, 11 kHz) are written as `<file>.d/room_NN/digi_NNNN.wav` (1,281 sounds in 65 rooms of `FREDDI.(a)`). Images (`AWIZ`), animations (`AKOS`) and scripts (`SCRP`) are not extracted. |
| **Freestyle Games `FSAR` archive (`.FAR`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | DJ Hero `Wii/FAR/*.FAR` (BE `FSAR`, version 2, data offset, entry count, 0x120-byte entries with a backslash path, sizes, offset and method 2 = zlib) → `<file>.d/<original path>`, extracted recursively (Granny `.gr2`, `.msb`, `.img`, `.wld`, `.cent`). Verified: 621 files in `3DOptionsMenuSystem.FAR`. |
| **Tron Evolution Battle Grids `*_` LZ files** | Wii / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | Every LZ10-packed file on the disc carries a trailing underscore (`light_ramp.tpl_`, `rta_1.brta_`, `x.brres_rmdl_`, `fontconfig.txt_`); the decompressor now drops the `_` so the inner TPL/BRRES/BRTA/text type is recognised (`light_ramp.tpl` → PNGs).; `wszst compress <file> -d <name>.<ext>_` (any extension ending in `_`) writes the LZ10 stream |
| **Disney Think Fast `.MIB` music** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `MUSIC/*.MIB` = DSP-ADPCM channel files stored back to back at 0x800-aligned offsets (each with its own 0x60 DSP header) → `<file>.d/chN.dsp` + `chN.wav` (mono 44.1 kHz per channel; `BT.MIB` = 2 channels, 84 s). |
| **Infinity Ward `.iwi` image (v7, v11)** | Wii / Image | ✅ | ◐ | Call of Duty: World at War (version 7, 0x1c byte header) and Black Ops (version 11, 0x20 byte header) `images/*.iwi` (`IWi`, GX CMPR, LE width/height at 6/8) → `<file>.png` (single-level files only; verified on a World at War 640x480 screen). Version 9 files (Modern Warfare 3) use another layout and are not decoded.; the texture / banner (not the text table) takes an edited PNG on repack (header kept)|
| **Bass Fishing `.u8c` text / Backyard Sports `.bank` sound bank** | Wii / Text, Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `text/game.u8c` (`U8C0` container, `STRS` chunk of NUL-terminated UTF-8 strings for all languages) → `<file>.tsv` (index, pool offset, text; 1,170 strings in the Big Catch table); `audio/*.bank` (BE header, count at 0x10, `{offset, size}` table of complete DSP-ADPCM files) → `<file>.d/NNNN.dsp` + `.wav` (verified on `BallBounce.bank`). |
| **Another Code R `.MESS` text** | Wii / Text | ✅ | ❌ | `REVOLUTION/**/MESS/*.MESS` (BE count, record offsets, records of `{id, a, b, c, length, UTF-8 text}`) → `<file>.tsv` (32 character profiles in the English `CHARA.MESS`). The same folder's `.BMG` files are standard BMG. |
| **Cabela's `TextLib.<lang>.bkp` text library** | Wii / Text | ✅ | ❌ | `Language/TextLib.*.bkp` (`TLIBv1.0d`, LE header with id/offset tables, pool of NUL-terminated UTF-16LE strings) → `<file>.tsv` (file offset, text); the tables are not interpreted, every NUL-terminated Latin text run is listed (2,401 strings in the English file of Cabela's Dangerous Hunts 2011). |
| **Phineas and Ferb `.lcl` localisation** | Wii / Text | ✅ | ❌ | `Global/<lang>.lcl` (BE record count, 24-byte records of hashes and offsets, pool of NUL-terminated UTF-8 strings: dialogue, keys, names) → `<file>.tsv` (index, file offset, text; 2,165 strings in `american.lcl`). The records are not interpreted. |
| **Yard Sale Hidden Treasures `.fez` data pack** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `data.fez` of *Yard Sale Hidden Treasures: Sunnyville* and the other WiiWare games on its engine (`FEZC`, little-endian: version, file count, name table and index offsets; `{u32 length, path}` names and one 32-byte `{type, offset, stored size, unpacked size, hash}` record per file). Type 2 records are zlib streams that end in a sync flush (no final block, inflated until the unpacked size) → `<file>.d/<path>` (3,345 files in the 31 MB `data.fez` of Sunnyville; fonts, `.gvf` scripts, UI and images). |
| **Simple Series Vol. 3 `.pk3`** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `*.pk3` of *Simple Series Vol. 3: The Mahjong*: an 8-byte header (two words, not interpreted) and one plain payload: a U8 archive, a BRRES or a TPL → `<file>.d/<file>.arc` / `.brres` / `.tpl` (else `.bin`), then unpacked like any other file. |
| **The Amazing Brain Train `.blk.bz2`** | Wii / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | `data/blockfiles/*.blk.bz2` (WiiWare): `{u32 1, u32 unpacked size, u32 unpacked size, u32 stored size}` and one bzip2 stream → `<file>.d/<name>.blk` (2.5-5.7 MB each). The block contents are not decoded.; `wszst compress <file> -d <name>.blkbz2` writes the wrapper (and `-d <name>.bz2` a plain bzip2 file) |
| **Around the World `.PTX` / `.PTXH` textures** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | `Wii/**/*.PTX` is raw GX texture data and the sibling `.PTXH` (80 bytes, `PATX WII`) holds its data size, width, height and format (`1TXD` = CMPR, ` 3A5` = RGB5A3, ` 565`, `ABGR` = RGBA32); the base level is wrapped in a TPL → `<file>.d/<file>.tpl` and `.png` (268 of the 270 textures of the WiiWare game; the other two are empty placeholders). A PTX without its `.PTXH` is left alone. |
| **Mix Superstar `.mxs` samples** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `*.mxs` (WiiWare): a 0x20- or 0x40-byte chunk table in front of one standard GameCube DSP-ADPCM stream (the 0x60-byte DSP header is located by checking samples/nibbles/loop-end consistency, 16 kHz mono) → `<file>.d/<file>.dsp` and `.wav` (816 of 816 samples of the game). The chunk table is not interpreted. |
| **Military Madness: Nectaris `.sir` streams** | Wii / Compression | ✅ | ❌ | `streams/*.sir` (WiiWare): a small `{id, size, previous stream size, next id}` row table, then `ZBLK` blocks (`u32 index`, stored size, unpacked size, zlib stream; the index restarts at 0 for each stream, 65,000 bytes per full block) → `<file>.d/<file>.<n>.bin`, one file per stream (four per file: 614,568 / 4,394,088 / 1,220 / 1,668 bytes in `L12_A.sir`). The unpacked level data (cameras, floats) is not decoded. |
| **Stunt Cars `.epk` pack** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `game.epk` (WiiWare, `IGEL*epk`, little-endian): `u32 version, u32 end of entries, u32 count`, entries of `{u32 unpacked size, u32 stored size, u32 path hash, u32 flags}` plus a raw deflate stream, and a directory at the end of the entries (`{tag, hash, unpacked size, stored size + 16, flags (low 16 bits = padded path length), entry offset, backslash path}`) → `<file>.d/<path>` (all 295 entries of the 30 MB pack, 60 MB unpacked: AI scripts, NDP/BLOC/VEHI data, JPEGs, `.NDS` audio). The contents are not decoded; the tool then mistakes the `.NDS` audio for Nintendo DS ROMs and reports a pass-through error. |
| **Moki Moki `.pacz` (LZS v5)** | Wii / Compression | ✅ | ❌ | `*.pacz` (WiiWare): `LZS\0`, version 5, offset shift `S`, unit size in bits (8, 16 or 32), offsets of the flag/token stream and the literal stream, unpacked size, file size, minimum match length. Flag words (u16 BE, 16 flags, lowest bit first) select a literal unit (1) or a match token (0: offset = `token >> S` units, length = `(token & (2^S-1)) + min`); ported from the game's own decoder and checked against the stream boundaries (token stream ends where the literals start, literals end at the end of the file) → `<file>.d/<name>.bin`, then unpacked as an `add` pack. A header with the chained-part flag is not handled. |
| **Moki Moki `add` pack** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | The content of a `.pacz` (`add\0`, version `0x04010000`, big-endian: entry table offset and size, count, total size; entries `{data offset, size, 0, path hash, name offset}` and NUL-terminated names) → `<file>.d/<name>` (178 files in `common.pacz`: `.tgz` textures and fonts, `.tpl`, `.brsar.bin`; the `.tgz` files are not tarballs and the tool's 7z pass-through reports an error on them). |
| **Hydroventure `.lpf2` level packs** | Wii / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | `Levels/*.lpf2` (WiiWare): `LevelPackV2\0`, version string `00020001`, big-endian `u32` unpacked size of an LZ77 layer, `u32` unpacked size of a Huffman layer, a `u32` that is not used by the decoder, data at 0x20. As in the game's loader the data is Nintendo BIOS compression in two layers (Huffman 8-bit, then LZ77 on the result); both sizes are checked → `<file>.d/<name>.bin` (1.3-2.5 MB of level data per file, not decoded).; `wszst compress <file> -d <name>`.lpf2` (the unused word at 0x1c is written as 0) |
| **Shark 3D `.s3d_pack` packs** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `rvl_packfile.s3d_pack` (Aya and the Cubes of Light, WiiWare): the text `shark3d_packstream_ident`, a version string and three big-endian `u64` values (index offset, end of data, 24) start the file; from 0x1000 on the file is made of 0x1000-byte pages whose last 4 bytes are a checksum, and the other bytes go through a fixed 256-byte substitution table (an involution, the one in the game's pack reader). In the decoded stream each entry is `path with every byte inverted, NUL, u8 type, little-endian base-128 length (top bit marks the last byte), payload`; type 1 is stored, 2 and 3 are zlib, 0 is empty. Entries are written under their paths in `<file>.d` (about 3600 files: TGA/PNG textures, WAV, `s3d_*` model, script and shader data; the `s3d_*` formats are not decoded). The `.stk` stacks of Babel Rising are a different format (next row). |
| **Shark 3D `.stk` stacks** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `S3DMain.stk` (Babel Rising, WiiWare): `06 08 00 00 00`, eight spaces, a 4-byte XOR key (0x0d), little-endian `u32` entry count and `u32` end of the directory (0x19 header). The directory (from 0x19, XORed with the key repeated from its first byte) holds `u32 path length, path, u32 offset (from the end of the directory), u32 stored size, u32 unpacked size, u32 checksum, u16 4` per entry; each entry's data is XORed with the key from its own first byte and is a zlib stream (stored when the sizes are equal) → entries under their paths in `<file>.d` (851 files: Lua bytecode, TPL/DDS textures, HUD/XML/SND/AIM data; contents not decoded). |
| **Where's Wally `.GWVideo`** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `*.GWVideo` (WiiWare): seven big-endian `u32` `{8, kind, 15, 0, 1, 0, 28}` and then the frames, each preceded by a few header bytes and a type byte, as complete PNG files or as `FMPP` frames (`.ppm`, 40-byte header with the payload size at 0x20). The frames are cut out by signature (PNG up to its IEND chunk, FMPP by its size field) → `<file>.d/frame_NNN.png` / `frame_NNN.ppm`. The FMPP frames are decoded by the `.ppm` row below. |
| **Where's Wally `.ppm` (FMPP) images** | Wii / Image | ✅ | ◐ | `*.ppm` (WiiWare) and the FMPP frames of `.GWVideo`: `FMPP`, `00 11 01 00`, `u8 2, u8 has-alpha, 8, 1`, `u32`, three big-endian `u16` width/height pairs (image size, size padded to 8x4 tiles, a third size), `u32` unpacked size, `u32` packed size, `u32` 40. The packed data is a FastLZ level 2 stream (leading bits `001` in its first byte) that unpacks to `u32 id, u16 512, u16 512, u32 offsets of two 512-byte palette planes (R,G / 0,B), u32 size + u32 offset of an alpha image, u32 size + u32 offset of the colour image`; the images are 8 bit in 8x4 tiles (colour = palette index, alpha = value) → `<file>.d/image.tga` (32 bit RGBA), `image.png` and `unpacked.bin`. An edited `image.png` is written back into the `.ppm` (colours matched to the 256 entry palette, alpha plane rewritten, repacked as literal-only FastLZ); `.GWVideo` frames are not rewritten. |
| **Line Attack Heroes `.alb`** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `ai_data.alb`, `s0NN.alb` (WiiWare): a 0x60-byte wrapper (twelve zero bytes, names, sizes) around one block that starts with a 3-letter tag. A `BAI` block holds `"BAI\0", u32 0x14, u32 first section offset` and the offsets of three more sections (counted from the block start, each behind zero padding) → `wrapper.bin`, `bai_header.bin`, `section_0..3.bin`; stage maps (`tpb`/`BAR` blocks) → `wrapper.bin`, `block.bin`. The contents (floats, hashes, tile indices) are not decoded. |
| **`LMPK` / `LMP2` `.ngr` files** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | `.ngr` of many DS puzzle and casual games (Jewel Legends, 5 in 1 Mahjong, 100 Classic Games ...): `LMPK` + `u32` unpacked size + zlib stream, or `LMP2` + the 13-byte header of a raw LZMA stream (props, dictionary size, `u64` unpacked size) + data; the stored size is checked → `<file>.d/<name>.bin` (not decoded further).; `wszst compress <file> -d <name>`.ngr` (LMPK/zlib variant only) |
| **`PACK` LZ10 files** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | `.set`, `.fnt`, `.bin`, `.def`, `.ftb` ... of about 37 German-published DS games (My Riding Stables, Bibi & Tina, Oktoberfest ...): `PACK`, `u32` flags (0 or 1), `u32` unpacked size, `u32` the same size (or twice it), then one or more Nintendo LZ10 streams on 4-byte boundaries, decoded until the size is reached → `<file>.d/<name>.bin` (+ `<name>.tail` for trailing bytes); `PACK` files with other flags (e.g. the 25 of `.fpk`) and text files that merely start with `PACK` are left alone.; `wszst compress <file> -d <name>`.packlz` (flags 0, one LZ10 stream) |
| **`SLBT` banks** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ❌ | `.atb` of the Battle of Giants engine (about 80 DS games): `SLBT`, `u32` unpacked size, `u8` flag (0 raw, 1 Nintendo LZ10), `u16` chunk count, `count * u32` chunk sizes (`size << 8`), a pad byte, then (LZ10 only) `u32` payload size and one LZ10 stream that must unpack to exactly the stored size → `<file>.d/<name>.bin` plus `.4bpp.ncgr` / `.8bpp.ncgr` tile sheets (16 tiles wide; the bit depth is not in the file); `DTBT` variant left alone. |
| **CRI `.ahx` / ADX audio** | DS / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `0x8000`, `u16` copyright offset, `u8` encoding at 4, `"(c)CRI"` ending at offset + 4. Encoding `0x10`/`0x11` (AHX) carries MPEG layer II frames → `<file>.d/<name>.mp2` (ffprobe verified, 22.05 kHz mono). Encoding 3 (ADX ADPCM: 18 byte frames, 4 bit, scale `& 0x1fff`, high pass coefficients from the cutoff at 0x10) → 16 bit `.wav`; the ADPCM filter was checked only by waveform smoothness, not by ear. 8/9 sample files convert (the 9th is zero filled). |
| **`RCMN` / `RAMN` (NITRO MultiCell)** | DS / Graphics | ✅ | ❌ | `.nmcr` cell banks (`KBCM`: cell table `{u16 oams, u16 oams, u32 offset}`, 8 byte OAMs) and `.nmar` animation banks (`KNBA`: animation `{u32 frames, u16, u16, u32, u32 offset}`, frames `{u32 pose offset, u16 duration, 0xbeef}`) → `<file>.d/<name>.json` structure dump; layout checked on the 4 magic-carrying samples of each (sums/offsets consistent), field meanings of the OAM words are not decoded. |
| **`swdl` (Procyon DSE sound bank)** | DS / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `.swd`: 0x40 (0x50 for version 0x415) byte header, 16 byte aligned chunks `wavi` / `prgi` / `kgrp` / `pcmd` / `eod `; each `wavi` entry (0x40 bytes: id +2, format +0x12, rate +0x20, pcmd offset +0x24, loop start/length in words +0x28/+0x2c) → `<file>.d/<name>_<id>.wav` (PCM8, PCM16 or SWAV style 4 bit IMA ADPCM). 41 samples from the 2 sample banks converted, waveforms smooth (not compared by ear); `.sed`/`sedl` sequences and `prgi` programs are not decoded. |
| **`DST1` sprite sheets** | DS / Graphics | ✅ | ✅ | `.dst`: `DST1`, flags, `u16` width/height at 0x0c/0x0e, `u16` palette size at 0x10, RGB555 palette (color 0 transparent), then linear 2/4/8 bit rows (bpp from the palette size, file size must match) → `<file>.d/<name>.png`. Verified on the one sample carrying the magic (FIFA 08 digits, renders cleanly); the other `.dst` files lack the magic and are not handled. |
| **`sedl` / `smdl` (Procyon DSE sequences)** | DS / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | `.sed` sound effect banks: `u16` offset table at 0x40, per effect a 0x30 byte info block (tpqn at +2, track count at +6) and `trk ` chunks of DSE events (note / pause / octave / tempo / program / volume / expression / pan; other events skipped with the parameter length table of the Project Pokemon "DSE SMDL Format" document) → one type 1 `<file>.d/<name>_<n>.mid` per effect (69 of 70 effects of the 2 sample banks convert; every track must end exactly on its `0x98`, so a mis-sync is rejected, not emitted). `.smd` songs (`smdl` with a `song` chunk) share the code but no sample was available to test it. Not checked by ear; infinite hold notes (`0xffffff` duration) are written with their long duration. |
| **`MOEG` geometry** | DS / 3D | ✅ | ❌ | Battle of Giants engine `3d/geometry` files (extension-less, ~1,500 per game): `MOEG`, `u32` size, then a raw DS GX display list (packed command bytes + parameter words; `BEGIN_VTXS` triangles / quads / strips, `VTX_16`, `VTX_10`, `VTX_XY/XZ/YZ`, `VTX_DIFF`, `TEXCOORD`) → `<file>.d/<name>.obj` with texel texture coordinates. All 1,456 files of Battle of Giants Fight for Survival parse to the end; 300 sampled convert without error; wireframes render as coherent rock/cave meshes. Matrix commands are ignored (parts placed by them stay at the origin); colors/normals not exported. |
| **`GRPH` graphics (EA DS: Boogie, Littlest Pet Shop, EA Playground, Football Academy)** | DS / Graphics | ✅ | ✅ | `.dat`: `GRPH`, `0xfffe`, size, blocks `GPAL` (first palette index at +0xc, 15 bit colors from +0x10) and `GIMG` (`u16` width/height at +8/+0xa, bits per pixel at +0xf; linear rows, or 8x8 tiles when +0xe is 6) → `<file>.d/<name>.png` (an edited PNG is written back into its `GIMG` block or, for a sprite frame, its pieces, quantised to the `GPAL` colours; index 0 transparent; one `GIMG` per last `GPAL`). Sprites (+0xe = 0x14) are a frame size plus OAM attr0/attr1 pairs that place one tiled `GIMG` piece each. Checked on all 666 Littlest Pet Shop: Garden files (867 images) and the Boogie background; the channel order (red in the high bits) was picked by eye. |
| **`NSC` tile maps** | DS / Graphics | ✅ | ❌ | `.nsc` / `_nsc.bin` (about 20 games): `NSC\0`, `u16 0x0300`, `u16 0x0100`, `u8` width, `u8` height (tiles, 32 x 24), `u16` 0, then `width * height` standard DS screen entries; size is exactly `12 + 2 * w * h` (3 samples) → `<file>.d/<name>.nscr` (a standard `RCSN`, picked up by the existing NSCR pipeline). |
| **`JBOA` animation manifests** | DS / Image | ✅ | ❌ | `.aap` next to an `.atb` and a `TTLP` palette: atb path, palette paths, then animations of frames of DS OAM objects (`attr0/1/2` as `u16` triples: y, x, shape/size, tile index in 32 byte units, 16 color bank). Each frame is composed with the palette (8 bit tiles when it has 200+ colors) and written as `<name>_<anim>_<frame>.png`; 80 of 84 manifests of *Battle of Giants: Fight for Survival* render (3 with rotation/scaling objects are skipped). Raw 8 bit data is read with wide/tall swapped, which is an empirical rule from two samples and still leaves some pieces wrong. |
| **NSBTA / NSBMA** (`BTA0` / `BMA0`) | DS / Animation | ✅ | ❌ | NITRO-System texture SRT animation (`SRT0` block) and material color animation (`MAT0` block); LZ10-compressed files are unpacked first. Per animation and material, five channels (scale S/T, rotation, translation S/T, or diffuse, ambient, specular, emission, alpha) are decoded as constants or per-frame samples (scale/translation 4.12 fixed point, rotation as sin/cos, colors RGB555, alpha u8) → `<file>.d/<name>.json`. Channels that carry fewer keys than frames (SRT flags `0x80`/`0x90`/`0x00`, MAT0 flags 4/8) are listed as `unsupported` with their raw fields. 166 of 178 sample files convert; the 4.12 scaling of the samples is inferred. |
| **Video containers (identify only)** | Video | ◐ | ❌ | Formats the [mobipeg](https://github.com/quatric/mobipeg) FFmpeg fork reads, recognised by magic plus a header sanity check and otherwise passed through unchanged (archive members are written as they are; nothing is decoded here): Bink 1 `.bik` (`BIKb`-`BIKk`) and Bink 2 `.bk2` (`KB2`), DPG `.dpg` (`DPG0`-`DPG4`), HVQM4 `.h4m`, MIVF `.mivf`, RVID `.rvid`, MobiClip `.mo` (`MOC2` / `MOC3` / `MOC5`), 3DS BPK1 `.bpk`, Flipnote Studio `.ppm` (`PARA`), FastVideo DS `.fv` (`FVDS`), VX `.vx` (`VXDS`); THP and Capcom MODS were already known. Use `mobipeg` to convert them. Formats without a usable magic (MOFLEX, TiVo TY, KWZ, GBA videos, MKV/AVI/3GP based ones) are not detected. |
| **NITRO raw graphics** `.nbfc` / `.nbfp` / `.nbfs` | DS / Image | ✅ | ◐ | Headerless tile data (4 or 8 bit), BGR555 palette and `u16` tile map (32 wide, flips and palette bank) that share a base name (about 1,800 DS games); a `.nbfc` is rendered with its `.nbfp` (required) and `.nbfs` (optional, otherwise a 32 tile wide sheet) to `<file>.d/<name>.png`; the same raw triple named `.char` / `.pal` / `.scr` in a few games is handled the same way (one sample set checked). Bit depth is inferred (4 bit for 16 color palettes or banked maps, else 8 bit). Checked on four sets (e.g. *New Super Mario Bros.* logo, *Licensed by Nintendo*), not on the whole census. |
| **NITRO raw 3D textures** `.ntft` / `.ntfp` / `.ntfi` | DS / Image | ◐ | ◐ | Headerless DS GX texture dumps (about 3,600 games): texels, BGR555 palette and (for 4x4 block compressed textures) block indices. The texture format comes from the palette size and the presence of `.ntfi`, and the width is searched (smoothest picture among 8..1024 wide, at most 4:1), so most pairs give a recognisable picture (photo, brick wall, tile atlas) and the rest are wrong width guesses. The 4x4 block palette offset is a count of 32 bit words (it was read as 16 bit entries, which sampled only the first half of the palette and turned every block compressed texture into noise). A `.ntft` with its `.ntfp` is written to `<file>.d/<name>.png`. Edited PNGs are re-encoded for every layout including the 4x4 one (rewritten `.ntfp` + `.ntfi`; a PNG whose width differs from the true one gives a scrambled texture). |
| **`RLE!` blocks** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ✅ | `.til`, `.msk` and others of about 40 DS games: `RLE!`, `u32` unpacked size, `u32` packed size, `u32` 0, then bytes where a control below `0x80` copies control + 1 literals and one with bit 7 set repeats the next byte (control & `0x7f`) + 1 times; files can hold many blocks → `<file>.d/<name>.bin` or `<name>_<n>.bin` (+ `.tail`). Every block must unpack to exactly its stored size (a `.msk` gives 98,304 bytes, a `.til` 288 blocks); what the unpacked data is (tiles, masks) is not decoded.; re-encode with a single block via `wszst compress <file> -d <name>.rle` |
| **Tagged chunk files** (`NIMG` / `ANIM` / `NFID`) | DS / Image, Animation, Font | ◐ | ❌ | One DS engine (about 40 games, e.g. *Successfully Learning English*): `{magic, u32 size}` plus `{tag, u32 size, data}` chunks. `NIMG` (`.img`): `IHDR` {format 1, width, height} and `CPXL` = one LZ11 stream of width x height RGBA pixels → `<name>.png` (checked on one sample, a button sprite). `ANIM` (`.ani`) and `NFID` (`.fnt`) → `<name>.json` chunk tree (u32 words and embedded text); sprite frame and glyph meaning is not decoded. |
| **`TKLT` tile sets** | DS / Image | ✅ | ◐ | `.atk` of the Battle of Giants engine: `TKLT` (raw) or `VNKT` (*Battle of Giants: Dragons*, `u32` size + Nintendo LZ10 stream), `u16` 4 bit and `u16` 8 bit tile counts, a palette path (`2d/palette/...`) and 4 bit 8x8 tiles; with the `TTLP` palette found under the file's own `2d` folder, a 16 tile wide sheet is written to `<file>.d/<name>.png` (four minimap tile sets of *Fight for Survival* checked; they show terrain tiles). The `.apl` playfield maps that use them are not decoded. |
| **`VNFP` playfields (`.apl`)** | DS / Image | ✅ | ❌ | *Battle of Giants: Dragons* tile maps (49 of 50): `VNFP`, `u16` 3, `u16` flags, tile set path, 5 bytes, then records `{01, kind, 01, u16 w, u16 h, ...}`; kind 5 = BG layer `{u8 layer, 0, u8 8-bit flag, u32 size, LZ10 map of NSCR-style entries}` drawn with the `.atk` tiles and `TTLP` palette to `<name>_bgN.png` (4 bit tiles from character 1, 8 bit tiles from character 512). Kind 1 records and the older `D2FP` playfields use a codec not identified yet (`28 xx xx 00` header) and are not decoded |
| **`FIGA` resource packs (`game.dat` + `game.fat`)** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Ubisoft *Petz* DSiWare titles and other Battle of Giants-engine games (~33): `FIGA`, then `{u32 size, tagged resource}` members end to end (tags stored reversed: `MECD`, `BMP `, `TEXL`, `TEXT`, `PLTT`, `MESH`, `GEOM`, `GO3D`, `FONT`, `LANG`, ...); `game.fat` = sorted `{u32 name hash, u32 offset}`, hash `h = h * 33 + c` over the lower-case path. Paths are recovered by hashing the strings of the pack and of the ROM's `arm9.bin`/overlays (1405 of 2534 in *Petz Hamsterz: Family*) → `<file>.d/<path>[.<tag>]`, the rest under `unnamed/<hash>.<tag>`. Member payloads are not decoded further |
| **`DST\0` image banks (`.dat`)** | DS / Image | ✅ | ✅ | Japanese quiz/trivia games (~19; *Gakken M Bunko: Monoshiri Edo Meijin*, *3-Pun Tenka Touitsu*): entries `{"DST\0", "1.7\0"/"1.00", u32 entry size, u16 w, u16 h, u32 palette bytes, 0, u32 pixel bytes, palette/pixel offsets, name[24]}` + RGB555 palette + linear 2/4/8 bit pixels (low bits first) → `<file>.d/NNN_<name>.png` |
| **`SSAM` archives (`.dat`)** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Square Enix DS (*Final Fantasy III / IV*, ~15 titles): `SSAM`, `u32` count, `{u32 offset, u32 size, char name[32]}` records, offsets from the end of the table → `<file>.d/<name>` (Nitro `NCGR`/`NCLR`/`NANR`/..., `*.lz` = LZ10) |
| **`DSARC FL` / `DSARCIDX` archives (`.dat`)** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Nippon Ichi DS (*Disgaea DS*): `u32` count, then (`DSARCIDX` only) `u16` ids padded to 4, then `{char name[40], u32 size, u32 offset}` → `<file>.d/<name>` (`.imy` images, `.msnd`, `.mpb`, tables); a repeated name gets `_<n>` unless its data repeats too |
| **`STCM2L` scripts (`.DAT`)** | DS / Script | ✅ | ❌ | Visual novel bytecode (*Uwasa no Midori-kun!!*, ~5 titles): after `CODE_START_`, actions `{u32 call, u32 opcode, u32 params, u32 size, 12-byte params, data}`; strings `{0, dwords, 1, bytes, Shift-JIS}` → `<file>.txt`, one `<opcode>: text \| text` line per action (210 = line, 212 = speaker in Midori-kun) |
| **PX compression (`AT4PX` / `PKDPX`)** | DS / Compression | ✅ | ❌ | *Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers*: 9 control nibbles, `u16` (AT4PX) / `u32` (PKDPX) decoded size; literal / back reference (hi + 3 bytes, 12 bit distance) / nibble pattern (control nibble k: four `lo` nibbles, slot k-1 = lo-1 for k 1..4, slot k-5 = lo+1 for k 5..8, direction settled on *Explorers of Sky* portraits) → `<file>.bin` |
| **PMD pack files (`.bin`)** | DS / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers* `dungeon.bin`, `monster.bin`, ...: `u32 0`, `u32` count, `{u32 offset, u32 size}` records, a zero record, `0xff` padding → `<file>.d/NNNN.<at4px\|pkdpx\|sir0\|bin>` |
| **`NCCL` / `NCCG` / `NCSC` (.ncl / .ncg / .nsc)** | DS / 2D Graphics | ✅ | ◐ | NITRO-CHARACTER source files of about 100 DS games (10 Second Run, Prinzessin Lillifee, A Ressha de Ikou DS ...): little-endian blocks `PALT` (BGR555 colors), `CHAR` (4 or 8 bit tiles) and `SCRN` (tile map), followed by `ATTR`/`LINK`/`CMNT` blocks → rewritten as standard `<name>.nclr` / `.ncgr` / `.nscr`, which the existing Nitro 2D conversion turns into PNG; an `.nsc` also converts its sibling `.ncl` / `.ncg` into the same `<name>.nsc.d/` folder so the combined `<name>.png` is rendered. Re-encoding: an edited `<name>.ncg.d/<name>.ncgr` (or `.nclr`) is copied back into the `.ncg` CHAR (`.ncl` PALT) block when the size is unchanged (8 bit PNG-import tiles are packed back to 4 bit); `.nsc` maps are not written back. `NCOB` (`.nce`, cell data) files have the same block layout; only their embedded `CHAR` tiles are exported (as `<name>.ncgr`, 16 tiles wide), the `CELL` / `ANIM` blocks are not decoded. No public documentation of these magics was found. |
| **Pokémon Battle Revolution big-endian `CRAN` NARC** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `arcDat/*.narc` use the byte-swapped `CRAN` magic with a `FF FE` byte-order mark and big-endian sizes (the Nintendo DS `NARC` is little-endian); the BOM now decides the byte order instead of the magic alone → `<file>.d/file_NNNN.bin` (277 files in `be_seq_rev.narc`). |
| **Simple Wii Series `.dpk`** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `data.dpk` of *Simple Wii Series Vol. 1-4 and 6* (D3 Publisher), little-endian: `u32 table end, u32 node count, u32 file count`, then path nodes (`objdata\avatar\boy\models\all`; a node that ends in `{u32 bundle offset (0x800 aligned), u32 bundle size, u32 parent, u32 0 or 8, u32 file count}` is a bundle) and one `{u32 part offset, u32 unpacked size, name}` record per file, assigned to the bundles in order. A bundle is `Wpbb`, `u32 part count`, `{u32 offset, u32 size}` per part; a part is a `Wp16` stream (`u32 unpacked size`, 32 flag bits then 32 words: 1 = literal word, 0 = codeword `dddddddddddccccc` copying c+2 words from d words back, history zero filled) or stored data. The LZ scheme is the one documented for the 3DS *Final Fantasy* ports; all five retail packages (155-526 files, BRRES/U8/TPL members) extract and the BRRES members parse. `datahead.dpk` (Vol. 4) is a table without data and is left alone |
| **Slam Games `.atd` / `.sad`** | Wii / Audio bank | ❌ | ✅ | `SLAM AD\0` banks of *Tele 7 Jeux* and *Margot's Word Brain* (`gamesfx`, `gamemusic`, `soundds`; the `.sad` copies are identical), little-endian: `u32 0x30000, u32 4`, chunks `{tag, u32 size, payload}`: `DFTR` (FILETIME), one `AUDO` per sound (`u8 1, u8 3, u8 0, u8 name length, u32 samples, u32 rate, u32 blob size, u32 loop end?, u32 blob offset, name`) and one `ADAT` whose first `u32` + 2 is where the blobs start. A blob is a little-endian DSP header (`u32 samples`, 16 coefficients, gain, ps, hist1, hist2, loop ps/hist1/hist2; 50 bytes) plus DSP-ADPCM frames; each sound becomes `<name>.dsp` (then WAV). The 129 s music track decodes to a smooth 22.05 kHz mono signal. The sibling `.std` texture banks (`SLAM TD\0`, one `IMAG` chunk `{u16 width, u16 height, u32 0, u16 format, u16 7, u32 0, u32 size, name, PGF image}` each; 20 files per disc) are split into `<name>.pgf` files; the PGF wavelet codec itself is not decoded |
| **`resource.dat` + `resource.tbl` (LZ11)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Deer Drive* and a sibling title: a `dummy`-headed `.dat` (0x20 bytes, then 0x20-aligned blocks) indexed by an LZ11-compressed big-endian `.tbl` whose entries from 0x20 are `{u32 offset, u32 compressed size, u32 unpacked size}` per slot (zero = unused). Blocks are LZ11 or stored; all 446 used slots of *Deer Drive* unpack (116 MB: U8, BRRES and textures) and are written as `<slot>.bin`, since the table carries no names |
| **Hash `PAK\0` (`!ZLS`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Kid Adventures: Sky Captain* `packfile/packfile.dat` (41 MB, 90 members; also in a sibling title): big-endian `PAK\0`, `u32 count`, `{u32 name hash, u32 absolute offset, u32 size}` records sorted by hash (algorithm unknown, so files are named `<hash>.bin`). Members are stored or `!ZLS` + little-endian unpacked size + zlib; all 88 `!ZLS` members inflate to exactly their recorded size. The unpacked payloads (`maxa`-tagged and `P\x02\x00\x00` files) are not decoded |
| **Pet Vet `.dat` packages** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Pet Vet: Marine Patrol* `wii/*.dat` and *Paws & Claws: Pet Vet* (`TAPSeaWii` / `Tierarztpraxis` as the text in the first 0x20 bytes), big-endian: `u32 count @0x20, u32 0x10000 @0x24`, then 0x80-byte entries from 0x40: backslash path (0x68 bytes), `u32 offset, u32 stored size, u32 flag (1 = zlib), u32 0, u32 unpacked size, u32 0`. Members keep their paths (`data/wii/animations/*.gr2`, ...). All members of the six `.dat` files tried (18-545 entries each) inflate to their recorded size; Granny `.gr2` payloads become `.glb` via the Granny handler |
| **Granny 2 `.gr2` (big-endian)** | Wii / 3D model | ✅ | ❌ | RAD Granny files with magic `B5 95 11 0E 4B B5 A5 6A 50 28 28 EB 04 B3 78 25` (*Paws & Claws: Pet Vet*; 32-bit pointers, uncompressed sections). Read generically: the section relocation tables resolve pointers and the embedded type definitions give each object's layout, so `Meshes` / `VertexDatas` / `TriTopologies` / `Materials` are walked by name. Written as `.glb` (one primitive per material group; positions, normals, UV0, vertex colours; bind pose, no skinning; a diffuse texture is attached when `<tga stem>.png` exists beside it). 1,029 of the 1,409 files in the sampled game convert; the other 380 are animation-only (no meshes) and are written as a generic `<name>.json` dump of the whole object tree instead (TrackGroups with their curve data: knots and controls, variant types resolved). |
| **Pet Vet `.wii` textures** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ◐ | *Paws & Claws: Pet Vet* `*.wii` textures: magic `12 B9 B0 A1`, `u8 0x65, u8 alpha flag, u16 width, u16 height, u8 format (2 RGB565, 3 RGB5A3, 5 CMPR), u8 bits per pixel`, 7 more header bytes, GX-tiled pixels from 0x13; `.wii1` ... are the mip levels (not decoded). Written as `.png`; 1,273 of the 1,275 base-level textures in the sampled game decode. The Granny `.gr2` converter also decodes the matching `<dir>_textures/<name>.wii` into the GLB. |
| **`PK03` hash package** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Deer Drive Legends* `Game.pak` (40 MB, 1,582 members) and `GameStatic.pak`, big-endian: `PK03`, `u32 0`, `u32 count`, `{u32 name hash, u32 offset, u32 size << 8 \| flags}` records sorted by hash (algorithm unknown, so members are `<hash>.bin`); flag 1 = LZ11 stream (all 1,446 flagged members start with `0x11`), 0 = stored. Unpacked payloads include Lua 5.1 bytecode (171), `XPrt` files (295) and unidentified tables; none are decoded here |
| **Ubisoft SB (`.sb7`)** | Wii / Audio bank | ✅ | ❌ | Sound banks of *Red Steel* (version 0x00180006-8, 1,146 files; layout after vgmstream `ubi_sb`): the internal hardware DSP-ADPCM sounds (mono and stereo, with loops) of the resource headers are written as `NNNN_<id>[_chN].dsp` (mini DSP header from the extra section, subblock-adjusted offsets), which `xx` decodes to WAV. 438 sounds from 191 banks are sample-exact against vgmstream. Streamed Ubisoft IMA sounds (external `.ss7` next to the bank, version-5 stream header) are decoded natively to `NNNN_<id>.wav`; 56 sampled mono/stereo streams are sample-exact against vgmstream. Software PCM sounds, sequences, layers and the `.ls7` / localised streams (not checked) are not extracted |
| **Sumo `.abc`** | Wii / Audio library | ✅ | ❌ | DSP libraries of *Sonic & SEGA All-Stars Racing* (116 files, 2,344 streams): complete DSP-ADPCM files back to back, each followed by 1..0x100 padding bytes up to a 0x100 boundary; split into `NNNN.dsp`, which `xx` decodes to WAV. Every file chains exactly to its length |
| **Eurocom MUSX (SBNK)** | Wii / Audio bank | ✅ | ❌ | `File_<region>.000` of *Dead Space: Extraction* (`MUSX` v10, platform `WII_`, `SBNK` form; layout after vgmstream `musx`): the codec 0x13 sounds (mono and stereo, 250 MB, 71 sounds) have per-channel `NGCA` DSP headers and are split into `NNNN_<id>[_chN].dsp`, which `xx` decodes to WAV; all 71 are sample-exact against vgmstream. *The Mummy: Tomb of the Dragon Emperor* `audiousa.000` uses `SBNK` version 0x0b (count / table / data base at 0x824 / 0x828 / 0x840, codec byte 0x11 but NGCA data): its 184 mono DSP sounds are extracted the same way (layout worked out by hand, no reference decoder; ps byte and sample counts agree with the NGCA headers). Other codecs (DAT4 IMA, PCM) and the `ESPD` project file (`File_COM.000`) are not extracted |
| **Konami `.vas` (Wii)** | Wii / Audio bank | ✅ | ❌ | *Jikkyou Powerful Pro Yakyuu* `strbgm.vas` and *MLB Power Pros* `sound/strbgm.bin` (same container with a `.bin` extension and a `0x005a8aab` marker in page 0; 458 streams, 1,838 files; its `voice*.bin` banks use a different per-stream header and are not decoded) (0x800-page container: header page + per-stream header page with one DSP channel header per channel, data interleaved in 0x100-byte blocks): every channel becomes `NNN_chN.dsp` and decodes to WAV (all 228 streams of the Ketteiban bank and the 91 / 112 streams of *Next*'s `strbgm.vas` / `strbgm_g.vas`, where the stored size can be short and the next header page is checked; interleave checked by L/R correlation, no reference decoder for the Wii variant); the `voiceN.bin` banks of *MLB Power Pros* (1619 streams in `voice1.bin`) use the same pages, with an upper 16-bit value beside the sample rate in the stream header (the rate is the low 16 bits), and a few trailing pages after the last stream (an index table, not decoded); every stream there is a 245-byte, 490-sample clip, which is why a bank holds over a thousand tiny files |
| **Ubisoft Jade BigFile** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Jade/LyN engine `.bf` (`BIG\0`, plus XOR-obfuscated `BUG\0`; *Rayman Raving Rabbids 1/2/TV Party*, *Prince of Persia* (Wii), *Petz*, *TMNT*, *My Word Coach*, *Movie Games*): fat blocks of `{offset, key}` refs, file infos and directory infos give the full path tree; members are streamed out as stored (multi-GB safe); stripped names fall back to the key. Layout after Ray1Map, verified on retail `2r3_bin_wii.bf`, `pop3wii.bf`, `MP08.bf` |
| **THQ Australia pack** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `pack` data archives of the *Nickelodeon Avatar: The Last Airbender* games (`*_DATA.PAK`): big-endian header, 16-byte `{name_offset, offset, size, 0}` entries, NUL-terminated `data/...` names, 0x800-aligned stored payloads (inner `.pak` / `.rad` files unpack recursively); streamed from disk. Verified on all nine retail `*_DATA.PAK` of *The Last Airbender* |
| **2XL LPAK** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | 2XL Games RIFF `LPAK` resource packs (*SCORE International Baja 1000*: `ANIMS.PAK`, `BAJAINF.PAK`, `PARTICLES.PAK`, `PERMTEX.PAK`): one `LIST/LDAT` per member with `dir `, `file` and `str ` chunks; members are inflated (one zlib stream per 0x4000-byte block, block starts listed in the `file` header) and the `str ` path components become the directory tree. All 959 members of the four retail files verified (PNG signatures, unpacked sizes) |
| **Ubisoft ABE BigFile** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Magma-engine `.BF` / `.BBF` (`ABE\0`, version 4, or version 5 as in *Rabbids Party: Time Travel* `RxN.bbf` and *Just Dance 2* `WD2.BBF`, whose LZO members carry an extra leading `u32 1`; *Rabbids Go Home* `RGH.BF` / `RGH.wii.sns.BF` / `RGH.$hd$.bik.BF`, *Red Steel 2*): chain of 200-byte-record table chunks with a 0x20-byte payload header per member; stored members and LZO1X block members (u32 packed-size table, 256 KiB blocks) are streamed out, `<$shadow$>` references into sibling BFs are skipped. All 8,568 decodable members of the three retail files verified (BIK/WPF content, no size mismatches) |
| **Wii resource ARC (`wii\0`)** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Big-endian `wii\0` resource archives (*Petanque Master*, *Petanque Pro*, and six more titles; `*.ARC`): 32-byte `{type, size, offset, id}` records plus an absolute name-offset table; offsets are relative to the data base in level/menu archives and absolute in the `*SOUNDS.ARC` banks. Members come out as `<name>.t<type>`; streamed from disk. All 105 retail files of *Petanque Master* checked | Ski-Doo Snowmobile Challenge flavour (190 of 198 ARCs): same records but the info block at 0xe0 gives the data base; type 2 images (BE u16 {w,h,pw,ph}, format at +0x0c, pixels at +0x60) are also decoded to PNG; variant 1 (header word 3 = 1: sound banks) is carved to `NNN.dsp` (GC DSP-ADPCM); variant 2 (`MUSIC.ARC`, 254 MB: absolute offsets, raw BE s16 mono PCM, rate not stored so 44100 Hz assumed) is written as `.bin` plus `.wav`.
| **PACK v2** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Little-endian `PACK` version 2 `.pak` (*Ski and Shoot* / RTL Biathlon 2009 `packfile.pak`, 2,848 members): 0x18-byte `{size, size2, offset, name_offset, crc, flags}` records with absolute `dir/file.ext` names. Stored members (flag `0x30`) are extracted; flag-`0x77` members use a custom byte-aligned LZ/bit coder that is not decoded yet and are written raw as `<name>.packed`; streamed from disk |
| **Sega ARCB / AVLZ** | Wii / Archive + Compression | ✅ | ❌ | Amusement Vision `ARCB` archives (*Super Monkey Ball: Banana Blitz*, 417 of its 426 `.arc`): 0x20-byte wrapper, a standard U8 tree whose node offsets/sizes (bit 31 = packed flag) address one `AVLZ` block; `AVLZ` is Okumura LZSS (4 KiB zero-filled ring from 0xfee, LSB-first flags, 2-byte matches with length 3-18). All 417 retail archives (8,335 members) unpack; bare `AVLZ` files are decompressed to `<name>.bin` |
| **IGA** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | Alchemy/Vicarious Visions archive (`.arc`, *Skylanders* Spyro's Adventure/Giants; *Trap Team* is version 11: no names, `{hash}` list then 16-byte `{offset, id, size, flags}` records, flags `-1` stored or `0x20000000` chunked LZMA with little-endian chunk sizes; members written as `<hash>.fsb`/`.igz`/`.bin`; version-2 `.bld`/`.pak` from *Madagascar: Escape 2 Africa*); members flagged `0x1000000N` are chunked LZMA (u16 BE size + 5 props bytes per 0x8000 unpacked, 0x800-aligned, incompressible chunks stored raw): `IGA\x1a`, hash table, `{offset,size,flags}` records and a build-path name pool; stored members are extracted with their original paths (`.igz`/`.fsb`/`.enc` payloads stay raw). | **STRT** | Wii / Text | ✅ | ❌ | One Piece *Unlimited Adventure/Cruise* string table (`event/msg/*.bin`, `*.str`): `STRT`, language tag and absolute string offsets; dumped to `<name>.txt` by `wszst xx` (strings kept in the game's own encoding). Placeholder 12-byte tables are ignored when empty |
| **MRQZ** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *My Fitness Coach* `.pak` (all 565 retail paks) and *Cosmic Family* `.mrq` (version 1: fixed 0x1000 header, 4 KiB-aligned members, `DATA/Minigames/...` paths; the `.DMCT` textures and the 74-byte `.snh`/`.snb` sound members inside are not decoded): `MRQZ` header, 76-byte `{name[64], offset, size, 0}` records and a raw data block aligned to the header size; members keep their `data/...` paths |
| **GCT0** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ✅ | Grasshopper Manufacture texture (*No More Heroes* 1/2 `.BIN`): `GCT0`, GX format, width/height and plain pixel data at 0x40 (I4/I8/IA4/IA8/RGB565/RGB5A3/RGBA8/CMPR); decoded to `<name>.png` by `wszst xx`, mip levels ignored; edited PNG re-injected on repack (header kept, mip chain regenerated)|
| **FUAX** | Wii / Text | ✅ | ❌ | *Rebel Raiders: Operation Nighthawk* string table (`text/<lang>/*.bin`, 262 files): `FUAX`, language tag, count, byte offsets and NUL-terminated UTF-16BE strings; dumped to UTF-8 `<name>.txt` by `wszst xx` |
| **STGS** | Wii / Text | ✅ | ❌ | *MySims Agents* string table (`Text/*.str`, 396 non-empty files): `STGS`, version 5, hashed keys with offsets into a UTF-8 pool; dumped to `<name>.txt` as `[hash]` blocks by `wszst xx` |
| **WB-L10n** | Wii / Text | ✅ | ❌ | *So Blonde: Back to the Island* string table (`Text/*.dat`, 425 files): `WB-L10n\0`, count and little-endian absolute offsets to UTF-8 strings; dumped to `<name>.txt` by `wszst xx` |
| **FCAT** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Winning Post 7 / World* track container (`.dat`, 63 files): `FCAT`, count and big-endian `{offset,size}` pairs; members (mostly BRRES) are extracted as `NNN.brres`/`NNN.bin` and peeled further |
| **EA SCHl** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | Electronic Arts `SCHl` stream (`.ngc`/`.str`, 144 files over 8 games, e.g. *Tiger Woods PGA Tour 12*): the EA ADPCM audio is decoded to `<name>.wav` through the ffmpeg/mobipeg pass-through by `wszst xx`; the interleaved VP6 (`m6PV`) video is not extracted |
| **EA SCHl EA-XA** | Wii / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | Native decoder for EA `SCHl` streams carrying EA-XA ADPCM (codec 0x0a on the GC/Wii and generic platforms, v1 with history or revised v2 / v3 frames with PCM frames; *The Godfather: Blackhand Edition* `.exa`, 10,577 files) to `<file>.wav` with `xx`; ffmpeg mistakes these streams for EA R2. 310 sampled streams (one stereo) are sample-exact against vgmstream; other SCHl codecs still go through the ffmpeg route |
| **WII! textures** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ◐ | *Star Trek: Conquest* `.WII` texture-only bundles (143 of 145 files: Splash, FrontEnd, levels, ...): `WII!`, version 2, count and a table of 0x18-byte `{name, w, h, kind, size, offset}` records; RGBA8, RGB565 and (best guess) RGB5A3 textures decoded to `<name>.d/NN_<name>.png` by `wszst xx`. | **PTEX** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ◐ | *Virtua Tennis 2009* (and *Sonic & Sega All-Stars Racing*) `.dat` texture bundle (461 files): `PTEX`, 0x20-byte records with padded size and offset; CMPR colour plane plus a separate I4 alpha plane (kind 2) or plain CMPR (kind 0), decoded to `<name>.d/NN_<hash>.png` by `wszst xx`. Kind 3 records are not decoded |
| **GFMC** | Wii / Text | ✅ | ❌ | *Teolsil Kirby Iyagi* (Kirby's Return to Dream Land) `message/<lang>/*.bin` (140 files): `GFMC`, label-hash table, offset table and a UTF-16BE string pool with control tags; dumped to UTF-8 `<name>.txt` as `[hash]` blocks by `wszst xx` (control tags dropped, entries without text left empty) |
| **PlatinumGames DAT** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | `DAT\0` archives (*Star Fox Zero*, *MadWorld*): big-endian offset/ext/name/size/hash tables with fixed-width names, extracted (and rebuilt) through the SFZ DAT scanner in `lib-sfzdat.c`; the 782 MadWorld `.dat` files share the same header layout |

| **TPLC / GFX** | Wii / Texture | ✅ | ◐ | *10 Minute Solution* LZSS container (`0xff`-led flag bytes, 4096-byte zero-filled ring, 4-byte big-endian decoded size): `.tplc` decodes to a TPL and then `<name>.png` with `wszst xx` (an edited `<name>.tplc.png` is re-encoded into the TPL's first image and the container rewritten with literal-only LZSS; `.gfx` / `.xpf` are not re-encoded); other payloads (`.gfx` Scaleform movies) are saved as `<name>.dec`. `.xpf` (magic `0xBADBEEF1`, no size) decodes the same way to `<name>.dec` |
| **HOG** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `.hog` archives (*Ultimate Band*: `u16 1, u16 2, u32 0x20`, count, then `{name_off, data_off, size, hash}` entries, backslash paths, uncompressed). | **Namco NUB** | Wii / Audio bank | ✅ | ❌ | *Family Ski*/*Family Jockey* `.nub` sound banks (big-endian, version `0x00020100`, table of `IDSP` stream header blocks plus one shared data region): every mono stream is written as a standard `.dsp` (decoded to WAV by `xx`); stereo streams are skipped |
| **SPD / SPT audio bank** | Wii / Audio bank | ✅ | ❌ | *Worms: Battle Islands* and Ubisoft Imagine banks (*Beastly*, *Babysitting Party*; an optional sibling `.snd` of 0x40-byte names names the streams) `.spd` (raw DSP-ADPCM frames) with its `.spt` index (big-endian: `u32 count`, `count × 28-byte {0, rate, 0, 0, end_nibble, start_nibble, 0}` records, `count × 46-byte` coefficient/history blocks): each stream is written as a standard `.dsp` (decoded to WAV by `xx`). Streams whose last frame is cut short behind the end nibble (*Burger Bot*) are zero-padded. | **Pipeworks bundle** | Wii / Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Merv Griffin's Crosswords* `.bdg` (`"Pipeworks bundle v1.03 (big endian)"`): type-name table plus 12-byte `{offset, type<<24 \| size, name hash}` entries; members are written as `<hash>_<type>.txt/.bin` (names are not stored; zlib members such as the `Ifc` XML interfaces are inflated). | **EA Attrib vault (`.vlt` + `.bin`)** | Wii / Database | ✅ | ❌ | Gameplay database of *EA Playground*, *Celebrity Sports Showdown* / *Le Tournoi des Célébrités*, *FIFA 10*-*15*, *2010 FIFA World Cup*, *EA Sports Active* 1/2, *Skate It*, *NHL Slapshot* (`data/db/db.vlt`, `data/attrib/db.vlt`, `db/db.vlt`; 26 census games with a `StrE` `.bin`). The `.vlt` is a BE chunk list (`Vers`, `StrN`, `DepN` = dependency names `db.vlt` / `db.bin`, `DatN` records, `ExpN` = `{u64 id, u64 type, u32 size, u32 offset}` exports, `PtrN` = 16-byte pointer fix-ups `{u32 location, u16 kind, u16 file, u64 target}`, kind 2 selecting the file the locations are in, kind 3 = pointer, file 0 = `.vlt`, 1 = `.bin`); the `.bin` is a `StrE` string pool followed by class field tables, arrays and strings. Keys are 64-bit Jenkins lookup8 hashes with level `0xABCDEF0011223344` (checked against the `DepN` names). `Attrib::ClassLoadData` = `{u64 key, u32 collections, u32 fields, ptr}` to 24-byte fields `{u64 key, u64 type, u32 size, u16 count, u8 flags (bit 0 array), u8 log2 align}`; `Attrib::CollectionLoadData` = `{u64 key, class, parent, u32 reserve, u32 shift, u32 entries, u16, u16 type count, u64 layout, u64 types[], 16-byte entries {u64 field, u32 value, u16 type index, u16 flags}}` (flag `0x4000` = value inline, else a pointer; arrays `{u16 count, u16 capacity, u16 element size, u16 pad}` + elements). `xx` writes `attrib.txt`: the class field lists, then every collection `[class/name] : parent` with typed values (ints, floats, bools, text, vectors, colours, RefSpec links, enums as numbers); `Structs::*` values are written as BE u32 words since the file does not describe their members. Names come from the `.bin` strings and the game's `sys/main.dol` (found in a parent folder): *EA Playground* resolves 30 of 32 classes and 426 of 585 fields. Output checked line by line against an independent Python decoder on *EA Playground* (908 collections) and *Le Tournoi des Célébrités* (650); also identical on *FIFA 10*, *FIFA Soccer 11*, *12*, *13*, *FIFA 14*, *FIFA 15* and *2010 FIFA World Cup* (93 to 415 collections each, no unresolved pointers), where the DOL carries none of the names, so FIFA keys stay hex (enum types such as `Material` print as one BE word); and identical on *EA Sports Active* (Personal Trainer, More Workouts), *Skate It* (`skater.vlt`, 3,238 collections) and *NHL Slapshot*. *EA Sports Active 2* and *NFL Training Camp* use a 32-bit-key variant (Jenkins lookup2, init `0xABCDEF00`, checked against `DepN`): every u64 key, count and fix-up target is a u32, so exports are 16 bytes, fix-ups 12, fields 16, collection headers 32 and entries 12; the width is told apart by the size of `ExpN` (4,297 and 6,736 collections, identical to the reference decoder); the *SimAnimals* / *MySims* `VALT` / `VBIN` vaults are a different format (next row); `tests/mk_eaattrib.py` (PASS) |
| **Sims vault (`VALT` `.vlt` + `VBIN` `.bin`)** | Wii / Database | ✅ | ❌ | Gameplay database of *SimAnimals*, *SimAnimals Africa*, *MySims Kingdom* and *MySims Agents* (`GameData/Vaults/*.vlt`, `Vaults/attribdb*.vlt`). Keys are FNV-1 32 over the lowercased name; names come from the strings of the vault folder's `.bin` files, the game's `sys/main.dol` (found in a parent folder) and the vault's string table. `.vlt` = `{u32 1, "VALT", u32 version (2 SimAnimals, 3 MySims), u32 size, u32 class count}`; v3 adds a type table at `0x28` (24-byte `{key, u16 size, u16 flags, u16 align, u16 members}` then per type the member keys, member type indices and member counts; a member of a type with members is followed by that type's members again as `x.y`, describing the same bytes); then one runtime pointer per class and the classes: 32-byte header `{key, 0x20, u16 fields}` and 16-byte (v2) / 24-byte (v3) fields `{key, type, u16 size, u16 flags (0x10 = array), u16 count, ...}` (v3: type index and a count of trailing 8-byte records, the two word positions told apart by walking to the end of the file). `.bin` = `{u32 1, "VBIN", version 4/5, u32 collections @0xc, u32 strings @0x20}`, collections from about `0x140`: a header with key, class and parent (plus a vault key and 4 pad bytes in *SimAnimals*), `{u16 entries, u16 capacity, u16 data size, u16 data capacity}`, `capacity` 8-byte entries `{field key, value}` and the data; then the string table `{case-sensitive FNV key, offset}` + strings. The header width, pad and word positions differ between the games and are found by walking the collection chain until the string table checks out. Values of size <= 4 are inline, else `{u16 count, u16 data offset}`; array elements may instead be separate entries keyed by FNV continued over `[%05d]`. `xx` writes `attrib.txt`: class field lists, then every collection `[class/name] : parent` with typed values (ints, floats, bools, text, vectors, colours, RefSpec links, v3 structs as `{member=value}`, undescribed types and enums as BE words). Output checked line by line against an independent Python decoder on all eight retail vaults: *SimAnimals Africa* `asset` / `tuning` / `master` (2,828 / 930 / 1,215 collections), *SimAnimals* `attribdb` (2,407), *MySims Kingdom* `attribdb` / `attribdbz` (12,355 / 10,284) and *MySims Agents* `attribdbd` / `attribdbz` (29,098 / 29,077); no unresolved field keys, every class name and all but 4 collection names resolve. `tests/mk_simsvault.py` (PASS) |
| **Localisation string table (`.str`, UTF-16BE)** | Wii / Strings | ✅ | ❌ | *Freddi Fish: Kelp Seed Mystery* `lang_*.str` (6 of 6): BE `{u32 tag, 01 00 00 00, u32 count, count * {u32 offset, u32 byte length}}` then back-to-back UTF-16BE strings; written as `strings.tsv` (UTF-8, escaped). Strict layout check, so the other `.str` formats (Asura, EA SCHl) are unaffected; `tests/mk_langstr.py` (PASS) |
| **CHNK tree (`.scene`, `.object`, `.phn`, `.var`, `.cha`)** | Wii / Container | ✅ | ❌ | *Paws & Claws: Pet Resort* `TPWiiProj/**` and other *Tierpension* / *Gesundheitscoach*-engine files (233 of 233 sampled): `CHNK` + one root chunk, every chunk `{tag[4], u32 size incl. the 8 byte header, payload}`; containers are payloads (after an optional u32 count) that tile exactly into chunks, optionally ending with a 4-byte copy of the parent tag. Written as `tree.json` (nested tags / offsets / sizes, leaf payloads as hex); `PHNM` lip-sync leaves (all 861 `.phn` files of *Der Gesundheitscoach*; `{u32 1, u32 0}`, a first entry `{end, 0x4b, id}`, then `{start, end, 0x4b, id}` records, tail tag; the count in the root head words matches) are decoded to `phonemes: [[start, end, id]]`; `CAMS` leaves (32 of the 41 `.scene` files of *Paws & Claws*; `u32 2`, then 76-byte records `{char name[16], 3 floats (aspect 1.333, 0.5, far 300), 3x4 matrix}`, tail tag) decode to `cameras`; `NULO` locator leaves (`u32 2`, records `{name, 3x4 matrix}`; names are C strings padded with `0` characters to the next 4-byte boundary after the NUL) decode to `locators`, and `ENTY` entity leaves (3331 of 3331 in the *Paws & Claws* scenes; `u32 3, kind, 1`, name, scale, 3x4 matrix, bounding box min/max, then a tail of words and resource-path strings, tail tag) to `entity` (tail words stay hex); `HEAD` scene headers (all 76 in the *Paws & Claws* scenes: `u32 2`, float scale, padded scene name, then six words `3, a, 0, 0, b, c`; the fourth word is the `NULO` locator count, the rest are unidentified counts) decode to `scene`; `SPLN` leaves (both files that have one: `u32 2`, records `{char name[4], padded path of a `.spline` resource}`) decode to `splines`; version 1 `SPLN` leaves (the five `.spline` files, 20 chunks: `u32 1`, `char name[4]`, `u32 point count`, `count x float xyz` path points, tail tag) decode to `spline`; `BSTR` BSP trees (all 37 in the scenes; `u32 2`, 6 bounds floats, counts `a` nodes / `b` leaves / `c` indices and a byte size, then a `BSPD` chunk `{u32 3, s32 children[a][2], {count, first}[b], u32 indices[c]}`; checked: every leaf range lies inside the index list, every child id is valid and the leaf counts add up to `c`) decode to `bsp` (the split planes are not stored; the meaning of the indices (triangles?) is not confirmed); up to three leading head words are allowed; `tests/mk_chnktree.py` (PASS) |
| **Momotarou Dentetsu `cmps` archive** | Wii / Container | ✅ | ❌ | *Momotarou Dentetsu 16* `data/{field,object,player,boot,event_new}.bin` (all 326 `event_new.bin` blobs decode): `cmps`, u32 method (2/4/5 share one decoder, found by tracing the loader in the DOL with Ghidra), u32 output size, then an LZ with 32-bit big-endian flag words (MSB first; 1 = literal, 0 = 2-byte match with a length nibble (0 = next byte + 0x12, else nibble + 2) and a 12-bit distance minus 1); the output is a table container `{u32 count, 3 zero words, count x u32 offsets}` whose members are `MC` / `TA` / `NB` / `CB` blobs. Writes `data.bin` and `NNNN.bin` members (blob subdirectories for the sector-aligned multi-blob `event_new.bin`); the same table container stored uncompressed (`field_tex_*.bin`, `vehicle.bin`) is split into members too; `TA` members (all 25 textures of `field_tex_a.bin`: `{"TA", u16 1, u32 size, u32 format 4 = C4 / 5 = C8, width, height, mips, ...}`, RGB5A3 palette, GX tiled indices, 0x58 byte trailer, several back to back) decode to PNGs (`<file>.NN.png`); `tests/mk_cmps.py` (PASS) |
| **Sony SCEI sound bank (`sound.bin`)** | Wii / Audio bank | ✅ | ❌ | *Momotarou Dentetsu 2010* `data/sound.bin` (15 banks, 167 samples, all Vagi counts match): little-endian PS2 engine banks in 0x800 sectors; the file starts `INIT`, each bank is a sector `BBNK` (BD offset u32 @0x1c) or `BANK` (BD offset @0x14) + `WAVE` + a PS2 `.hd` image at +0x40 (`SCEIVers`, `SCEIHead` with the BD size, `SCEIVagi` with {BD offset, u16 rate} entries, `SCEIPrgm` ...); the BD is PS-ADPCM (16-byte frames: shift / predictor, flags (bit 0 = end), 14 bytes = 28 samples, standard 60/115/98/122 filters). Every Vagi sample becomes `bNN_sNNN.wav`; the programs / key maps are not decoded; `tests/mk_sceibank.py` (PASS) |
| **Momotarou Dentetsu 2010 font sheet (`font.bin`, `font2.bin`, `kantei.bin`)** | Wii / Font | ✅ | ❌ | *Momotarou Dentetsu 2010* `data/font*.bin`, `data/kantei.bin` (all three checked by eye): a plain 1024x1152 GX I8 texture (8x4 tiles, 1179648 bytes, no header) holding a 32x32 pixel glyph sheet (32 columns x 36 rows; `kantei.bin` is a bold face); written as PNG. The glyph order is custom: `message.bin` (u16 BE glyph ids from 0x0110, control words such as `0c0f`) indexes it and is not decoded; *Momotarou Dentetsu 16* `font.bin` / `kantei.bin` (524288 bytes; the DOL loads `font.bin` and builds a 16 step alpha palette for a 1024x1024 texture) are 1024x1024 GX I4 (8x8 tiles) sheets with the same glyph layout, checked by eye; `tests/mk_momofont.py` (PASS) |
| **Imagine `.wsn` scene mesh** (Party Babyz `babyz/motion/*/base.wsn`) | Imagine-LZ wrapper, then BE {object / texture counts, offsets, size + format words}, objects -> meshes (0x30 header + 28 byte vertices {xyz, 2 unknown floats, RGBA}) as plain triangle lists, 16 bit tiled textures | → `model.obj` (vertex colours as extra `v` components) + `tex_NN.png` | extract | Implemented: layout recovered by tracing the game's scene loader in Ghidra; all 494 retail `.wsn` produce an OBJ (checked visually: the tutorial file is a Wii Remote and a hand with the right texture). UVs and skinning matrices are not decoded (`.wa2` animations are, see below). *Beastly* `base.wsn` uses a variant mesh header (index count, unique vertex count, ..., `+0x28` 28 byte records, `+0x2c` u16 indices, `+0x30` position array); the "huge floats" are real positions (about 1e5, scaled by bone matrices at run time), all 31 retail files produce an OBJ (traced from the game's GX draw loop). Beastly `Face.wmo` (inside the Imagine-LZ wrapper, so `wszst x` unwraps it): BE `{u32 n, float3 [n], float3 [4n], float3 [2n]}` (size `4 + 84 n`; the later arrays repeat and mirror the first positions, probably an expanded face-morph vertex list), meaning not decoded. |
| **Shrek Forever After `.apk` (APKF)** | Wii / Resource package | ✅ | ❌ | `APKF` + u16 LE version 0x0105, BE words: pool table (`IMG`, `DATA`, ...), groups (`TEX`, `MAT`, `MESH`, `SKEL`, `FONT`; each with a per pool alignment word list) whose items consume sizes sequentially from the pools. Layout recovered from the game's APK loader (Ghidra) and checked on all 365 files (41,928 textures). A texture is a 0x40 byte `GCNT` header {size, w, h, GX format, palette format, mipmap count - 1} + GX pixels (all mipmaps) + a palette for C4/C8 | → `tex_NNNN.png` (largest mipmap) | extract | Implemented for textures (CMPR, C8, RGBA8, RGB565, RGB5A3, ...); materials, meshes and skeletons are not decoded (a `MESH` item is an `IMG` pool descriptor + `DATA` pool arrays: indexed float3 positions / normals / UVs and a GX display list `0x98 <u16 count> (pos, nrm, uv u8 indices)...` for the vertex arrays; a name string table (u32 hash + NUL terminated name) follows the item table; the file ends with two relocation lists: pointer words are `pool << 26 | word offset` (pool 0x3f = end marker), the running pool offsets are shared by all groups; arrays and per vertex descriptors `{(1 << 16) \| count, stride << 8 \| 1, 0x80000000, ..., data pointer @ +0x1c}` plus `{attr << 24 \| stride, pointer}` records (attr 9 = position, 10 = normal, 13 = texcoord) are spread over the `IMG` pool, so mapping them to meshes needs the mesh struct (not worked out)); `tests/mk_apkf.py` (PASS) |
| **Imagine `.wa2` skeletal animation** (*Beastly* `odyssey/motion/*/anim.wa2`) | BE {clip count, bone count, bone offsets, clip offsets}; bone = {index, bind translation, child list}; clip = 0x18 byte header + per frame {u32 flag, float3 root motion, float3 second track, one unit quaternion per bone} -- layout from the game's anim loader (traced in Ghidra), confirmed by every clip size being exactly 0x18 + frames x (0x1c + 16 x bones) | → `anim.json` (bones with parents, per clip all tracks) | extract | Implemented: all 39 *Beastly* `.wa2` files parse and validate (quaternions are unit length). The clip names are the numbers in the sibling `anim_<name>.h`. The meaning of the "second track" (large smooth floats) is unknown. |
| **CRI USM movie (`.usm`)** (Sofdec 2) | `CRID` chunk stream (`@SFV` video, `@SFA` audio, `@ALP` alpha; 32 byte chunk headers with payload offset / padding / channel / type) | → `video<N>.m1v`, `audio<N>.adx` (+ `alpha<N>.m1v`) | extract | Implemented from the public chunk layout; verified only on a synthetic file (no retail Wii USM at hand). Masked (encrypted) video is written as stored. |
| **Momotarou Dentetsu 16 `message.bin`** (Hudson) | BE 16-bit glyph codes (`01xx`/`02xx`) after a glyph catalogue, control bytes `0c` (line), `0c 0f 00` (block), `0a XX` / `05 XX` -- glyph order recovered from the DOL's character tables in Ghidra | → `message.bin.txt` (kana, Latin, digits as UTF-8; kanji glyphs as `{gXXX}`) | extract | Implemented for the glyphs identified from the DOL (digits, A-Z, a-z, hiragana, katakana, space, `!`); kanji pages are dynamic and left as glyph ids, so the text is only partly readable. |
| **Pangya `.pet` / `.mpet` model** (Super Swing Golf) | BE chunk list (`BONE`, `MESH`, ...); MESH holds a `grff`/`rmg` container with vertex / index buffers (`vbd`, `vbh`, `ibd`, `ibh`), vertex elements (`ved`) and 0x20 byte primitives (GX strips, 0x98) -- layout from the game's MESH loader traced in Ghidra | → `model.obj` (one object per primitive, positions + UV + normals) | extract | Implemented: 19,875 primitives validated against their buffer sizes; 2175 of 2194 retail files produce an OBJ (the rest are `_ani` effects without meshes). Wing-shaped avatar accessory rendered as a sanity check. Rigged models also write `skeleton.txt` (bone names from `BONE`, parents from the rmg `mdl` nodes, local 4x4 matrices from `trns`; checked on a 161-bone avatar: Bip01 hierarchy, bone lengths along X). Skinned avatar meshes (no `ved`) use fixed 33 byte vertices {u8 bone, float3 position, float3 normal, float2 texcoord} (found by matching the primitive's stride 0x21 to the vertex buffer size; positions are bone-local, strips are single triangles joined by degenerates). Skin weights / bone transforms and the texture links are not applied. Ball models (no `ved`, 28-byte primitives, 32-byte `{pos, normal, uv}` vertices) also export. |
| **Pangya `.gsr` texture** (Super Swing Golf) | BE `ffrg`/`rsg` header, reversed-tag chunks `tex`/`texd` (+ `pal`/`pald` for C8 with a 32 bit ARGB palette); RGBA8, C8, CMPR | → `<name>.png` (level 0) | decode | Implemented: 3780 retail `.gsr` decode (checked visually on a CMPR atlas and a C8 sky sprite). The 9 little endian `.le.gsr` (`grff`) files are not handled. |
| **`.bcpak` resource pack** (Top Shot Arcade, Our House - Party!) | BE `9a3f` + count, hash, then {size, offset, hash, check} entries | → `NNN_<hash>.tpl/.bin` | extract | Implemented: all 449 retail packs parse; TPL entries are recognised by magic. |
| **FLCG collision (`.GCL`)** (No More Heroes) | BE (version LE) `FLCG`, linked models with origin, data record -> 48 byte triangles {next, 0, material, v0, v1, v2}; layout from the public GHM File Docs notes | → `model.obj` (object per model, `usemtl` per material) | extract | Implemented: all 45 retail `.GCL` (stage collision) produce an OBJ. |
| **GAN2 animation (`.GA2`)** (No More Heroes) | `GAN2` + 0x30 byte LE tracks {name, parent, child, prev, next, data offset} | → `tracks.json` (track hierarchy + per-channel raw samples) | extract | Partial: skeleton tree and channel tables decoded (ids 0-2 translation, 3-5 rotation; format 5 = s16 samples scaled by 2^(16 - parameter byte), 1 / 4 = float32); the scale is inferred (NAVEL height of walk / crouch / jump clips 10 / 5.5 / 26 vs bind height 11.2; rotations in radians), not confirmed in the game code; the frame ordering of some channels (jagged first samples) is not understood. |
| **GMF2 model (`.gm2`, `.gmf2`)** (No More Heroes 1/2) | LE objects / textures / materials tables, shared big endian vertex buffer, triangle-strip surfaces (11 / 9 / 13 byte vertices), embedded GCT0 textures (layout from the public GHM File Docs notes) | → `model.obj` + `model.mtl` + `<texture>.png` | extract | Implemented: positions accumulate down the parent chain, rotations are not applied (character bones are not posed); UV scale 1/1024 and normal scale 1/64 are inferred. All 510 retail `.GM2` produce an OBJ. |
| **RMHG container (`.rsl`)** (No More Heroes 1/2) | LE `RMHG` {kind, 0x20, build no.} + 32 byte entries {offset, size, nested flag, build, id}; nested containers recurse | → members under their real names (name table, XOR 0x8d, found by tracing the game's loader: `MODEL/TRV001S.GM2`, `MOTION/TRVA00100.GA2` ...), else `NNN_id.<ext>` (GCT0 → `.bin`, GMF2 → `.gmf2`, GAN2 → `.gan2`) | extract | Implemented: all 2195 retail `.rsl` extract. GMF2 / GAN2 contents are not decoded. |
| **KP0 package (`.pkt`)** (Power Punch) | LE `KP0` + count / data start / 24 byte entries {name, size, offset, flags, unpacked size}; members raw, LZ10 / LZ11 or RLE (BIOS headers) | → members by name (+ `references.txt` for path-only entries) | extract | Implemented: all 269 retail `.pkt` extract; nested packages extract in turn. |
| **GVX0 event script (`.pvx`)** (Power Punch) | LE `GVX0` v0x116, header {flag, 0x28, A/B/C/D section starts, size}; A = 8-byte nodes, B = 24-byte records, C = 16-byte records, D = parameter words (floats, -1, 0xfffffffe separators) + strings (brsar path, cue names, `.pkt` refs) | → `gvx.txt` (section word dump) + `strings.txt` | extract | Partial: all 73 retail `.pvx` dump; record semantics (timeline / event types) not decoded. |
| **RC chr fighter record (`.chr`)** (Power Punch) | 32 bytes LE: `RC\0\2`, u32 1, s16 -1, u16 0, 5 floats (look like body measurements; Dummy = all -1) | → `chr.txt` (f0..f4) | extract | Implemented: all 28 retail `.chr`; field meanings unconfirmed. |
| **RCB resource bundle (`.rcb`)** (Bass Fishing Wii / Big Catch) | BE: 0x40 header {0, 0x40, 1, name@0x20}, members behind 0x40-byte headers {span (aligned 0x20), 0x40, size, last-flag, path@0x20}; payloads are BRFNT / BRRES | → members under their stored paths | extract | Implemented: all 6 retail `sys_lang_*.rcb` (font + `sys_lang.brres`). |
| **WIS block-LZSS stream (`.wis`)** (Cabela's Big Game Hunter 2010 / Dangerous Hunts) | no magic, LE chain of blocks {u32 size (bit 31 = stored), data}, 0xffffffff end marker; blocks decode to 0x7ffc bytes with Okumura LZSS (4 KiB ring, zero-filled per block, start 0xfee) | → `<stem>.bin` payload + `lua/NNN_<name>.luac` (embedded Lua 5.1 bytecode scripts, ~2,000 across the maps) | extract | Partial: all 28 retail `.wis` decode; payloads are `<Cauldron Chunk File>` containers (maps, actors, weapons; a stream of items `ffffffff, flag, size` plus raw records; the GX textures inside are carved to `tex/*.png` and the Havok 6.1.0 packfiles to `hkx/*.hkx` (20 in sg2, 14 in act_str); chunk files with the standard footer (type-name table + 0x18-byte object records, layout traced in the game DOL's chunk loader) are split to `chunks/NNN/objects.txt` + per-object blobs (all 353 act_str chunks, incl. the older footer without version word used by `keyframes` animation chunks, decoded to `keyframes.txt`: per bone id hash, f32 position keys, s16x4 rotation keys (normalised on export, convention not verified) and u16 frame numbers; ids match the bone ids of the skeleton chunks; mesh blobs = 0x54-byte node header with bbox floats, name, material path, s16 BE vertices {nx, px, py, pz, ny, nz}, 2 pad bytes, s16 uv pairs, and 0x99 triangle strips of {vertex, normal, uv} u16 triples; each is also written as `NNN_mesh.obj` (plus a static `NNN_mesh.gltf`, buffer embedded, one triangle list with position / normal / uv, and for skinned meshes `NNN_mesh.skin.gltf` with the chunk's skeleton, one skin, inverse bind matrices and JOINTS_0 / WEIGHTS_0: a bone node header stores the WORLD position and the inverse of the bone's world rotation as quaternion, so child bones lie along the parent's x axis after `matrix(quat) * (p_child - p_parent)`; rest pose round-trips to 5e-5, vertices of finger / hand bones lie 1-3 cm from their bone; for blended vertices between a parent / child bone pair the weight of slot a falls as the vertex moves along the segment from a towards b (correlation -0.17 over 16,864 vertices), consistent with the weight belonging to slot a, though weak), 141 in act_str; positions are model-space fixed point /8192 and match the header bbox exactly, normals /8192, UVs /4096); node headers (id hash, parent id hash, pos / quat / scale, bbox) of mesh and bone objects are listed per chunk in `skeleton.txt`, parent ids resolve to bone names; each mesh also gets `OOO_mesh.skin.txt` (per vertex bone names and weights: C = rigid vertex groups {count, start, palette slot}, D = 8-byte blended groups {count, offset, start, slot a, slot b}, E = one weight byte per blended vertex; chain consistency checked on all 141 act_str meshes, weight-to-bone orientation assumed) and the OBJ header comment lists the mesh's bone palette (indices of bone objects, verified: a hand mesh lists the hand and finger bones)) or the GUI archive in `startup.wis` (BE hierarchical index; 105 BRLAN / BRLYT / BRFNT / TPL members extracted to `gui/`, the one TPL `defcursor_final64_a.tpl` whose header looked damaged was a decoder bug: the LZSS ring is initialised with spaces (0x20, as in Okumura's original), not zeros, which also fixed one item size word in act_str). |
