Materials without such a map (e.g. terrain-layer blends) stay untextured and keep their vertex colours. Skinned meshes become a glTF skin: attribute 4 holds four `{u16 palette joint, u16 weight/65535}` pairs per vertex, and the geometry's joint palette lists `{joint-name hash, 4×4 column-major inverse bind}`. PARC extraction writes `skeletons.tsv` (joint hash → `PSKS` skeleton). A `PSKS` skeleton holds joints `{name hash, u16 parent, translation, rotation quaternion xyzw (all-zero = identity), scale}`. The first skeleton holding every palette joint supplies the hierarchy; without one the palette joints are exported flat at their bind poses. Tangents, extra UV sets and the `CBuf` (attribute 41) data are not applied |
| **PANM (phx)** | Switch 2 / Animation | ✅ | ❌ | *Kirby Air Riders* animation clips: `Main` flatbuffer whose root table holds the last frame, fps, groups → targets (64-bit joint-name hash) → channels {property hash, value type, kind, key count, byte offset/size into a blob} and the blob's absolute file offset/size. Transform channels (value type 1: scale xyz, rotation xyzw, translation xyz) are either a 10-float constant or a quantized key stream: header {flags (1 scale, 2 uniform scale, 4 rotation, 8 translation, 16 flag bit), reference-transform offset, bits per key, data offset, key count}, nine {min, max, bits} component ranges, the reference transform, then per key LSB-first bit fields `min + (max−min)·v/(2^bits−1)` (zero-width components keep the reference) plus a sign bit for the rotation's derived w. Exported as a GLB holding the `PSKS` skeleton with the most animated joints (via `skeletons.tsv`) as a skin-only armature, with one linear translation/rotation/scale channel set per joint at the clip's fps. Property channels (value types 3 float, 8 bool with one bit per key, 9 float2: material, UV and visibility properties) are written to `<id>.channels.json` as `{fps, channels: [{target, property, type, keys}]}` with the key values decoded the same way |
| **engine_bin (phx)** | Switch 2 / Config | ✅ | ❌ | *Kirby Air Riders* `startup.dat` and `Bin/config.bin` are obfuscated with a byte chain, `plain[i] = AES_InvSbox[c[i] ^ c[i+1]]` (one byte shorter than the file). `startup.dat` then holds MessagePack values (a version, a directory → 128-bit digest map for `.nrr`, `Asset`, `Asset.Streaming` and `Bin`, and label → `engine_bin:/…` targets) and is written as a JSON array. `config.bin` (recognised only beside a `Prism_SB` `schema.bin`) is written as `config.prism`, the deobfuscated data for the game's Prism serializer, whose schema-driven layout is not decoded. `Asset.Streaming/*MV.bin` uses a different, still unknown cipher |
| **Sygnus soundstream / PSND** | Switch 2 / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | Bandai Namco SOL-AVES "Sygnus" sound engine (*Kirby Air Riders*): `.soundstream` streams (`SYGN` + `STRM`, AES-192-ECB encrypted as shipped and decrypted with the game's key) holding the engine's ADPCM (per-channel 512-sample blocks: two float seed samples, a step index and 4-bit codes into a 32-row table with a second-order predictor) → WAV, Nintendo Switch Opus → Ogg (+ WAV with ffmpeg), or RIFF. Sound effects are phx `PSND` records embedding a Sygnus `ASRC` bank with one ADPCM source → WAV; streamed sources (format 3) only reference a `.soundstream`. After Nenkai's 010 template and vgmstream issue 2063 |
| **IDSP (Inevitable)** | GC / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | Inevitable Entertainment `IDSP` (`.idsp`: Defender; DSP-ADPCM with 0x2e-byte coefficient records, block-interleaved) decoded to `.wav` via `wszst extract --decode` (after vgmstream `idsp_ie.c`); the Namco, Traveller's Tales and Next Level variants keep their own handler. Verified on a synthetic stereo file. |
| **NXOF / NXMS** | Switch / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | Nihon Falcom `.nxopus` (`nxof`, Ys X) and Arika `.nxms` (Tetris the Grand Master 4; one or two Opus segments) Switch Opus repacked into Ogg Opus and decoded to `.wav` with ffmpeg/mobipeg when installed via `wszst extract --decode` (after vgmstream `nxof.c` / `nxms.c`). Verified on synthetic streams. |
| **Metroid Prime RSF** | GC / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | Metroid Prime headerless `.rsf` stereo G.721 (32 kbit/s CCITT ADPCM, 32 kHz, one half of the file per channel) decoded to `.wav` via `wszst extract --decode` using Sun's public-domain G.721 reference code as vgmstream does (`raw_rsf.c`). Cross-checked against ffmpeg's G.726 32 kbit/s decoder on an ffmpeg-encoded stream: the first samples are identical and later samples stay close but not bit-identical (ffmpeg's G.726 differs slightly from the Sun reference); no real `.rsf` file was tested. |
| **Switch Opus** | Switch / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | Nintendo Switch Opus (`.opus`/`.lopus`/`.opusnx`, magic `0x80000001`, and the `OPUS` wrapper of NUS3AUDIO): packets repacked into a standard `.ogg` Opus file and decoded to `.wav` with ffmpeg via `wszst extract --decode` (layout after vgmstream `opus.c`). Verified with a stream rebuilt from an ffmpeg-encoded Ogg Opus. |
| **BWAV (native)** | Switch / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | Nintendo Switch `.bwav` (PCM16 and DSP-ADPCM, any channel count, loop points as `smpl`) decoded natively to `.wav` via `wszst extract --decode` (layout after vgmstream `bwav.c`); Opus-coded BWAVs (codec 2, *Ocarina of Time* Switch 2 voice lines: a `0x80000001` Switch Opus stream at the channel start offset) are repacked and decoded like a `.opus` file. Verified on synthetic PCM16 and DSP files with exact sample comparison. |
| **Rhythm Heaven Groove CWV** | Switch / Audio | ✅ | ❌ | *Rhythm Heaven Groove* `sound/sm/**.cwv` (2,209 files): sample data, then a 0x100-byte footer {u32 codec (2 mono, 3 stereo), u32 rate, u32 frames, u32 2, u32 loop start, u32 loop length, floats}. One byte per sample and channel (interleaved); each channel is a running s16 sum of a 256-entry delta table indexed by the byte, wrapping at 16 bits (codec and table from the game's loader, which expands to PCM16 before playback). Written as `<name>.wav` with the loop in a `smpl` chunk; all 2,209 decode. |
| **Rhythm Heaven Groove WMB** | Switch / Tempo map | ✅ | ❌ | `wavmark/**.wmb` (271 files): u32 0, u32 rate, s32, two u32, u32 0, then {u32 ticks, u32 end sample} segments and a terminating 0, 480 ticks per beat (every segment gives a whole-number tempo, matching the BPM in the tutorial file names). Written as `<file>.yml` with start tick/sample and bpm per segment. |
| **Rhythm Heaven Groove TEX / SPR** | Switch / Texture | ✅ | ❌ | `graph/**.tex` (1,159, zstd): a Tegra block-linear surface plus a 0x1000-byte trailer {u16 format (101 BC7, 99 RGBA8), u16, u16 width, u16 height, u16 mips}; block height is the largest power of two not above the height in GOBs (max 16), which reproduces every file's size. Mip 0 is written as `<file>.png`. The `.spr` beside it (u32 flags, 13 counts, 27 u64 section offsets) has its atlas rectangles {x, y, width, height} (section 0) written to `<file>.yml`; the other sections are not decoded. |
| **BARS (Switch 2)** | Switch 2 / Audio Archive | ✅ | ❌ | `wszst xx` unpacks every asset to `<file>.bars.d/<name>.bwav` + `<name>.amta` and then decodes the members in place (BWAV to WAV, AMTA to YAML). |
| **BEA (Switch 2 "SCNE")** | Switch 2 / Archive | ✅ | ✅ | *Switch 2 Welcome Tour* `romfs/Archive/*.ounce.bea`. A member that is also the directory part of another member's path (`layout.lyt` next to `layout.lyt/textures/...`) is written as `<name>.@file`; repacking strips the suffix. Members whose data lies past the end of a truncated dump, or is not a ZSTD frame, are skipped with a `BEA: skipping unreadable member` warning instead of rejecting the archive. |
| **FSAR 6.2 sound archive (.fsst)** | Switch 2 / Audio Archive | ✅ | ❌ | *Switch 2 Welcome Tour* `audio/sounddata/*/*.fsst` inside the `sound~subarc_*` archives: `wszst xx` unpacks the `.bfseq` / `.bfbnk` / `.bfwar` assets, converts sequences to MIDI and decodes the wave archives. |
| **NRR0 (Switch 2 module list)** | Switch 2 / Module hashes | ✅ | ❌ | `.nrr` (Welcome Tour `romfs/.nrr/tcn_Release.nrr`): signed header, then `{u32 hash_offset, u32 count, u64 0}` and `count` SHA-256 digests of whole `.nro` files (all 36 `nro/Ounce_Release/*.nro` digests are in it). `wszst xx` writes `<file>.txt`, one digest per line; the signature area is not parsed. |
| **AMTA 5.0** | Switch 2 / Audio metadata | ✅ | ❌ | Audio metadata of a BARS asset (*Mario Kart World*, 22,578 entries checked): header offsets (absolute) to the data block (0x10), markers (0x14), MINF music info (0x18), tags (0x1C) and parameters (0x20); name (relative to 0x24) and its crc32 (0x28), flags (0x2C), track and channel counts with a {channel, speaker} map (0x30). The data block is a field mask followed by the present fields: bit 0 peak and bit 1 RMS amplitude (both match the decoded wave exactly), bits 2/3 two dB levels, bit 5 a level envelope {u16 count, u16 kind 2, {f32 level, u32 sample}...}, bit 6 one more float. Markers {id, name, start, length}, tags {name}, parameters {name, u32}, MINF version 1.1 with sample rate, tempo and time signature (its bar tables are left undecoded). Written as `<file>.amta.yml`. |
| **AATN / AROC / AACL** | Switch / Audio attenuation | ✅ | ❌ | `.baatarc` (SARC) members: attenuator (five {curve name, curve type} pairs, directivity, culling, listener and occlusion flags), roll-off curve (model, ref/max distance, factor, start value, increase mode, v2 culling start) and culling (distance, margin, v2 priority-down flag), per Kinnay's Nintendo-File-Formats wiki; written as `<file>.yml`. |
| **AGST 10** | Switch 2 / Audio group settings | ✅ | ❌ | `.bagst`: section offsets after the header (TREE, GRP, MAND, PASE, GCCD, BFIL, STRG). The group tree (node size, group index, child count) is written with group names, groups with their parameter words, and the MAND/PASE/BFIL sections, which are BYML documents (ducking, pause and filter settings), decoded inline. |
| **DAPG** | Switch 2 / Acoustic probes | ✅ | ✅ | *Mario Kart World* `Sound/Acoustic/Probe/<scene>/<x>_<z>.dapg`: u32 grid size N (64), N*N u16 cell indices (0 = none, else record + 1) and N*N 0x7C-byte records (two floats, two u16, four floats, then 12 {f32 distance, u32 tag} rays in three groups of four); written as `<file>.csv`, one line per cell.; `wszst create <file>.dapg.csv` rebuilds the binary (needs rows in cell order and every record index present), byte-exact against the dump |
| **DLPG** | Switch 2 / Light probes | ✅ | ❌ | *Mario Kart World* `Env/<world>_X<x>_Z<z>_<level>.dlpg` (28,125 files): `DLPG`, u32 version 6, then columns of four probes (4 x 160 bytes + {f32 base y, three f32 gaps}); a probe holds 24 floats of lighting coefficients (8 x RGB), 4 + 4 floats, its x/y/z position, a u32 and 16 bytes of packed data. Every file size fits 8 + 656 x columns; positions step 31.25 along x/z. Written as `<file>.csv`, one line per probe. The other Env files (`.dlpl`, `.dlpa`, `.dalp`, `.bkls`, `.dcgm`, `.dlpr`) use their own layouts and are not converted. |
| **XLNK (XLink2)** | Switch / Effect & sound links | ✅ | ❌ | `.belnk` (ELink) / `.bslnk` (SLink) databases to `<file>.yml`: param defines, then per user (CRC32 name resolved from the name table) local properties, user params, asset call tables (asset params resolved from the direct value / string / curve / random / arrange / bitfield references, or switch / random / blend / blend-by / sequence / grid / jump containers with child ranges and the children's conditions), action slots → actions → triggers, property triggers and always triggers. All four layout families are handled (32-bit 0x1c/0x1e, ACNH 0x1d/0x1f, 64-bit 0x1f/0x22, 0x21+/0x24; ambiguous versions are tried both ways, the extension picks the first). Checked on *Tomodachi Life: Living the Dream* (`elink2.Product.100.belnk` 0x24, 1,366 users; `slink2.Product.100.bslnk` 0x22, 1,269 users): container, curve, random, condition and weight counts and all 46,575 scalar asset params match dt-12345/xlink2. The older layouts are untested on real files. |
| **BAEV (animation events)** | Switch / Animation | ✅ | ❌ | `.baev` (BFFH / BFSI "Nintendo.AnimationEvent" reflection data) to `<file>.yml`: per animation the node list, per node its events (name, id, hold flag) with triggers (frame) and holds (start / end), each with int / float / vec3 / string params. Animation keys are FNV-1a hashes; names come from the matching `Model/<name>.bfres` (`.zs` too) and, for AsNode archives, from the element GUIDs of `AS/<name>.asb`. Checked on all 185 *Tomodachi Life: Living the Dream* archives: 6,085 of 6,085 animation names and 1,194 of 1,194 AsNode keys resolve. Layout per dt-12345/baev. |
| **ASB (animation sequence binary)** | Switch / Animation | ✅ | ❌ | `.asb` version 0x41B to `<file>.yml`: commands, elements with their type-specific parameters (selectors, skeletal / material / shape / bone visibility animations, blenders, frame controllers, ...), child plugs with their conditions, blackboard (names resolved for bound properties), partials, partial skeletons, float property ex, tags, external actions, material blends and enum relocations. State, transition and bone blend records are only counted. Layout per the EPD wiki "ASB" article and dt-12345/asb, with corrections found on *Tomodachi Life: Living the Dream* data (Simultaneous / Event plugs at +4, absolute plug offsets, 8-byte PreviousTagSelector child plugs, float property ex {pointer, count}); all 31 files of that game convert. |
| **Phive cloth** | Switch / Physics | ✅ | ❌ | `.bphcl` (Phive header, Havok 2023.2 `TAG0` tag file, AAMP cloth parameters): the tag file is dumped through its own reflection data (types, members, inheritance, hkArrays, pointers as `@item`) to `<file>.yml`, the AAMP to `<file>.param.yml`. All 15 sample navmeshes convert. |

| **VoiceText database** | Switch / Speech | ✅ | ❌ | Third-party VoiceText TTS `.vtdb2` (*Tomodachi Life: Living the Dream* `VoiceText/tts_single_db(*).vtdb2`): {u32 count, u32 name size, u32 key, info} then {name, source path, u32 offset, u32 size} records; names and `.txt2` word lists are stored with every byte + key. | **Mario Kart World, not converted** | Switch 2 / Misc | ❌ | ❌ | Left as they are by `xx`: culling data (`Cull/**/*.bgrd` 8 KiB tables of 32-byte bitfield records, `.bcul`, `.bbox`, `.pccg`), time-trial ghosts (`.ghost`), the ATRA table in `Sound/Attenuation/SPLAttenuation.Product.100.baatarc`, compiled NVN2 font shaders (`nvn2_font_shader*.bin`), and the bespoke Env grids named in the DLPG row. | **AINB** | Switch / AI | ✅ | ❌ | AI node graph (`AIB `, versions 0x404-0x408; all 88 *Mario Kart World* files are 0x407). | **Phive static compound (.bphsc)** | Switch 2 / Collision | ✅ | ❌ | *Mario Kart World* `Phive/StaticCompoundBody` (21,224 files): Phive container 2.2 (`Phive\0` 02 02) with a u32 section offset table at 0x0C. About a fifth carry Havok 2023.2 tag files (`TAG0`; the INDX/ITEM table and TYPE/TNA1 + TST1 names locate every object) holding an hknpCompoundShape: 64-byte instances {quaternion, translation, scale, s64 relative shape pointer} place hknpMeshShape (same layout as `.bphsh`), convex polytopes (vertices, faces, face indices) and capsules (end points + radius), possibly through nested compounds; all of it is written as one world-space `<file>.bphsc.obj` (checked against the compounds' stored AABBs on 300 files). Water bodies (about a quarter) hold a grid {u16 height, u16 flow x, u16 flow z, u16 flags} under a {size, center, width, height} header and become `<file>.png` (16-bit heights), `<file>.flow.png` and `<file>.yml` (bounds and height formula). The rest are empty bodies. |
| **zstd dictionaries (`ZsDic.pack.zs`)** | Switch 2 / Compression | ✅ | ❌ | *The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time* (Switch 2) `*.zs` files are zstd frames that name one of four trained dictionaries by ID (1 zs, 2 bkprb, 3 bcett.byml, 4 pack); the dictionaries live in `romfs/Pack/ZsDic.pack.zs` (a zstd-compressed SARC of `*.zsdic`). `wszst` registers them when a file under such a romfs tree is processed (searching `Pack/ZsDic.pack.zs` and `romfs/Pack/ZsDic.pack.zs` in the parent directories) or from the `ZSDIC` environment variable (a pack, SARC or bare `.zsdic`). |
| **NSO (+ ZBIC segments)** | Switch / Switch 2 / Executable | ✅ | ❌ | `NSO0` executables (`.nso` or an unsuffixed exefs `main`): `xx` writes `<file>.d/text.bin`, `rodata.bin`, `data.bin`. Segments flagged compressed are LZ4 blocks, except in Switch 2 modules such as the *Ocarina of Time* (Switch 2) `exefs/main` (flags 0xff, module name `Owl`) where each segment is a **ZBIC** frame: a zstd frame whose magic is `ZBIC` (0x4349425A) instead of 28 B5 2F FD and whose FSE normalized-count headers (the Huffman weight table of every literals section and each FSE-coded sequence table) are stored by binary interpolative coding. Header byte = {bit 7 low-probability flag, bits 0-6 byte count R}, then R bytes read from the last to the first into a 48-bit rANS-style state (refill below 2^40): `charNum = x % 52 + 1`, `tableLog = x % 8 + 5`, the cumulative total, then the cumulative counts by interpolative coding over a power-of-two index tree (pre-order, right subtree first, each node a mixed-radix digit of span `hi - lo + 1`). Everything else is stock zstd. The reader (`ZBIC_readNCount` at the end of the FSE section of `libzstd/zstd.c`, switched on only while a ZBIC frame is decoded) follows kinnay/zbic (GPL-2.0); the three segments of the real `main` decode bit-exactly, matching the SHA-256 hashes in the NSO header. The regress case encodes a standard zstd frame into ZBIC with an inverse of the reader (`tests/mk_zbic_nso.py`). |
| **BFEVFL (event flow)** | Switch / Event | ✅ | ❌ | `.bfevfl` (`BFEVFL`, versions 0x0300 / 0x0400; layout after zeldamods `evfl`): actors (identifier, params container, actions, queries), events (action, switch with cases, fork / join, sub flow, sub flow switch, return) linked by event name, entry points with variable definitions and typed parameter containers, dumped to `<file>.yml` via `wszst xx`. All 914 *Ocarina of Time* (Switch 2) flows decode without errors; timelines are not decoded (the one sample header has a timeline count of 0). |
| **Zelda Switch 2 bake / culling / foliage tables** | Switch 2 / Misc | ✅ | ❌ | *The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time* (Switch 2), all written as `<file>.yml` by `wszst xx`: baked light lists (`Bake/Light/*.bklight`: `BKLT`, version 1, counts of point / spot / area lights, then 28 / 52 / 60 byte records of position, direction, up, colour, falloff, size, intensity, scale), culling boxes (`PreComputedCulling/*/Boxes.bbox`: version 2, count, 44-byte {id, flags, centre, three zero floats, size}; the low mantissa bits of the centre floats carry noise) and foliage tables (`.flnt`: impostor info with 256-byte pascal names, {first record, count, kind} ranges and 80-byte frame matrices; the 1168-byte node-tree variant is dumped structurally as node flags / packed bytes and record indices, its meaning is not known). 90 of 90 sample tables decode. |
| **MCPK (mesh codec package), container only** | Switch 2 / Model | ✅ | ❌ | *The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time* (Switch 2) `Model/*.bfres.mc`: `wszst xx` writes `<file>.yml` with the container layout (MCPK version 1.0.0, flags, section A starting with an `FMSH` header {version 0x10000, work memory size, two output sizes, 0x129}, section B; 28 + A + B equals the file size on all 5 samples). **The payload is not decoded:** it is not a zstd frame at any offset and not the 0.x layout of the public MeshCodec decoder (dt-12345/MeshCodec), which rejects major version 1, and both sections are incompressible. Not decoded either: `.baatarc` (ATRA attenuation table) and the NDVE `Movie/Ndve/*.dat` video streams (`NDVE` + `NDSR` stream table, custom codec). |
| **TARB (tera terrain)** | Switch 2 / Terrain | ✅ | ❌ | *Mario Kart World* and *The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time* (Switch 2) `romfs/TerrainArc/<scene>/` tiles (`.hght`, `.mate`, `.norm`, `*.extm`): 0x14-byte `TARB` header (u16 header size at 0x0A, zstd size at 0x10) over a payload whose 0x34-byte info block gives the grid (u16 BE, margins included), margin, per-channel types (6 = 8 bit, 9 = 16 bit, `08 08 07` = R11G11B10F), a delta-packed flag (0x08 & 0x80) and three self-relative {offset,size} blocks: data (plain or delta packed: nibble-split low bytes, per-row running sum, bias 8 / 0x78), aux, and a GPU copy (block linear, Switch 2 Orin GOBs, block height 1 GOB, full mip chain; BC1 = code 0x30, BC3 = 0x34). `wszst xx` writes `<file>.png` beside the source (16-bit gray for heights, RGB16 for water, RGB with reconstructed Z for normals), a Radiance `<file>.hdr` for the R11G11B10F `bake_light` layer and `<file>.alpha.png` when a 4th channel carries data. Delta-packed heights decode identically to the deswizzled GPU copy on every sampled tile. Layout cross-checked with TscbSharp's notes for *Tears of the Kingdom*. Ocarina of Time (Switch 2, TARB version 1, 260-texel grids) adds: type code `0x0b` = one R32F channel (heights as 0..1 of the scene `height_scale`, written as 16 bit gray), BC5 (`0x80 .. .. 0xc6`, `bake_ao`) and BC6H (`0xc8`, `bake_gi`, six 260x260 slices written side by side in one Radiance HDR), array slices (info byte 3; `.mate` has two) deswizzled one by one, and the block height taken from the slice size (260 texels need 16 GOBs). |
| **TSCB (MKW)** | Switch 2 / Terrain | ✅ | ❌ | Terrain scene version 0x090C: world scale (0x10), height scale (0x14), counts of layers/materials/areas (0x30/0x34/0x38) and self-relative tables at 0x70/0x74/0x78. Layer entries hold id byte, size, margin, mip count, format code (1 R8, 5 R16, 0x0A RG8, 0x1A R11G11B10F, 0x1D RGBA8, 0x27 RGBA16, 0x30 BC1, 0x34 BC3), default RGBA, flags and name/extension/class strings; areas hold x, z, size, name and the layer files they ship with height ranges. Dumped as `<file>.yml`. |
| **RESTBL** | Switch / Resource table | ✅ | ✅ | `.rsizetable` of *Tears of the Kingdom* and Switch 2 titles: `RESTBL`, version, name field size, crc and name counts, sorted {crc32, size} pairs, then {name, size} entries for colliding paths. `wszst xx` writes the RSTB-style text listing and, when the file sits inside a `romfs` tree, hashes every path in it (without `.zs`) to annotate entries: 184,690 of 235,879 *Mario Kart World* hashes resolve.; `wszst create <file>.rsizetable.txt` rebuilds the binary from the listing (and `<file>.rstb.txt` for the older RSTB), byte-exact |
| **BFTTF / BFOTF** | Switch / Font | ✅ | ✅ | NintendoWare scrambled TrueType / OpenType fonts: the first word is the scrambled signature 0x7f9a0218 (key = byte-swapped word ^ signature; Switch 0x49621806, Wii U 0x8cf2dcd9, PC 0xa6018502), the second the font size, then the font as scrambled big-endian words. `wszst xx` writes `<stem>.ttf`, or `<stem>.otf` when the payload starts with `OTTO` (CFF outlines, e.g. *Mario Kart World* `NinDev-RaceFontMoveR.bfotf`), beside the source.; re-encode with `wszst compress <file> -d <name>.bfttf` (Switch key) |
| **FCPX (complex font)** | Switch / Font | ✅ | ❌ | NintendoWare complex font description (`FCPX`, LE, version 9.3.0.0 in *Mario Kart World*): u32 font count at 0x10, then node offsets relative to 0x14. Node kinds: 0 bitmap font {name}, 1 scalable font {size f32 +4, alternate char +0x14, scales +0x24/+0x34, name +0x2C, code ranges count/offset +0x40/+0x44 as {first, last} pairs}, 2 pair {two child nodes}; all offsets relative to the node. Written as `<file>.yml`. |
| **BFFNT (Switch 2)** | Switch 2 / Font | ✅ | ❌ | Little-endian `FFNT` 4.x: the TGLP sheet data offset holds a complete BNTX (one texture per sheet, e.g. RGBA8 sRGB 1024x1024 in *Mario Kart World* `PicFont_00`), decoded through the BNTX path and flipped vertically (sheets are stored bottom-up) to `<file>.png` / `<file>.sheetNNN.png`. Switch 1 fonts with raw block-linear sheets fall back to a tiled decode that picks Maxwell or Orin GOB order by image smoothness. |
